## Aug 21–24 2026 — post-show: the factual-data doctrine, JVL Dollars defended, and the Flex-Port-Mini business model

Three sessions in this stretch. Substance lives on the topic and decision pages; the through-lines about Val himself:

- **He made "hypothetical vs factual" an explicit decision rule** on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-21-post-goam-show-recap.md|Aug-21 post-GAMOA recap]] and refused to change Flex IQ on show feedback — see [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Aug-21) and [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-release-flex-iq-as-is-place-units-collect-facts.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **He defended the JVL Dollars design against two of his own people** and won both rounds — [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-keep-jvl-dollars-printed-serialized-cards.md]] — arguing dollars rather than discounts, and that the physical package buys access to a CFO rather than a technician. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **He ruled the cell modem onto every shipped machine from September 1**, activated only against a signed data-sharing agreement — [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-cell-modem-on-every-machine-from-september-1.md]] — and made the same modem the price of a free software conversion — [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-convert-vertical-machines-instead-of-new-trials.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]
- **He came out of the Georgia show bullish and out of the sales conversation lost.** After pressing for September–November numbers he told [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] "I feel that you are completely demoralized," then left the meeting early: "I'm lost. I'm absolutely lost… My comments are not useful. I'm going to leave you alone, the two of you, and you'll probably speak a different language with each other. I'm probably in your way." He had walked Light & Wonder, Novomatic, IGT and Bulldog and concluded "we're in very, very good shape." Detail on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Aug-21). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]
- **He designed the Flex-Port-Mini business model himself** on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-24-flex-port-mini-capabilities.md|Aug-24 session]] — free hardware, a ~$65/month fee, the per-transaction dollar handed to the operator — and set the build budgets. He took on writing the player use-case and the operator business case in plain prose and then having Claude produce the capabilities document. [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-free-hardware-fixed-monthly-fee-model.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-flex-port-mini-via-second-sas-only.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **He declined to author the prose standard himself** on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-24-nova-lgs-prose-standard-checkpoint.md|Aug-24 prose checkpoint]] — "я мне кажется неправильный человек. Если попросить меня написать стандарт прозы, я, наверное, неправильно сделаю" — delegating it to Vladimir and Evgeny while keeping the end-of-September deadline and the monitoring for himself. He also declined to let Evgeny derive capabilities straight from code without prose: "может быть это можно, но я не хочу это делать." [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-single-prose-writing-standard.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_LGS_Prose_Standard_Checkpoint-August-24-2026.md]]
- **He stayed out of the engineering schedule deliberately**, handing day-to-day sequencing to [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] and [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]]: "я не буду лезть в ваш следжил… Они знают все наши приоритеты." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_LGS_Prose_Standard_Checkpoint-August-24-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear across all three sessions — market, device and programme economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 25 2026 — the credentials and the doctrine, told to a Georgia master

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-25-epic-amusement-kenny-patel-visit.md|Aug-25 Epic Amusement visit]] he met [[wiki/people/kenny-patel.md|Kenny Patel]] face to face and, before pitching, told him who he was: "I'm not here to talk about me. I'm here to talk about you, but you need to know about me." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]

- **The biography, CPI version.** "43 years in in the business… second generation. My father started the business… My two daughters are coming into the business." CashCode (transcribed "Cashcoast") was sold "in two thousand and six… to uh CPI… Crane National Vendor"; he sat on the Gaming Standards Association board "from two thousand to two thousand six" and "was reelected six times". A non-compete kept him out of gaming machines, so JVL made amusement countertops; after the sale he "turned JVL into a gaming manufacturer", and transactional equipment followed when that non-compete ended. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]
- **Not dictating the product.** "I am not here to dictate: 'Hey! You need to buy my ATM.'… this is the final destination. You don't have to do it." And, repeatedly: "I love bill acceptors… But the more profitable way of operating is removing bill acceptors." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]
- **Against counter-sent credits.** "screaming ten dollars on this machine… It never worked from the eighties. Belgium tried it. England tried it… we here in America tried it." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]
- **Asking for the order.** "Write your own deal… I want to walk away with an order." He did not get one. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]
- *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)*

## Aug 26–28 2026 — running the company from the Georgia office: pricing theory, the legal red line, and QA as the bottleneck

Six sessions in three days, all with Val chairing — four internal, two customer visits. *(A seventh, back-filled later: the Aug-28 ~9:50 AM office drop-in by a Texas operator — see the last bullet.)* Substance lives on the topic, project and decision pages; the through-lines about Val himself:

- **He ran the week from the Georgia factory floor after the trade show** — "я сейчас работаю с нашего офиса в джорджии… я сейчас на фабрике здесь" — and used the visit to walk a live location himself and photograph a JVL machine standing beside Light & Wonder and Aristocrat units. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He put forward a revenue theory and then refused to let it become a decision on the call.** His read: JVL's high RTP and 25¢ minimum bet mean the machines cannot make competitive netwin during peak hours however popular they are. He offered it as a hypothesis — "Это моя теория… я вам высказал свою гипотезу, я бы хотел бы, чтоб может быть вы посидели, подумали мне сейчас ответ не нужен" — and after [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]]'s alternative parked both: "давайте это я запаркую, потому что мы никогда сейчас не… Ни до чего не дойдем." Detail on [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]] (Aug-26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He named a communication failure as the main obstacle to his own NOVA plan.** Handing [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]] and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] the September capabilities work, he told them the hard part is not technical: "Вы или не умеете или не любите разговаривать? Из вас тяжело чтото вытащить… Хотя знание много," and demanded an education process rather than answers. He also told them to stop dividing the company into "Минск девелопмент и всё остальное" — "У нас всё одно." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-pavel-irina-own-nova-capabilities-to-jira-through-september.md]] and [[wiki/topics/minsk-team-communication.md]] (Aug-26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He treated the certifier's trust as a balance-sheet asset.** On self-testing on the Light & Wonder bench: "тот знак доверия, который мы сейчас получили. Нам нужно капитализировать его?" — because a sloppy self-test sends JVL back to a regime that is "значительно дороже" and slower. [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-self-test-on-lw-sas-bench-in-georgia-plus-minsk-emulation.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He drew an absolute legal red line with InComm, in cost terms.** On JVL's ATM ambition: it "cannot be implemented… Without us being licensed as an ATM operator. Which requires a bank," and "We are not in the business of breaking the law… because it's going to cost us probably our business and 43 year of tedious work. So we're not going to jeopardize it over a potential market in Georgia." He simultaneously refused to pretend the ambition away: "we are quite keen on the idea of becoming an ATM manufacturer." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-certify-only-flex-iq-and-flex-port-with-incomm.md]] and [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Aug-26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He volunteered full product transparency to a supplier as the price of trust.** "We're not being cute in the way we conduct our business… we're currently certifying only two products with you," and if the roadmap accelerates "you will be the first one to know." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **He asked for switching optionality specifically to avoid re-certification cost.** On multiple clearing houses in Flex-IQ: "we need to have this capability that we could easily switch on a fly. I don't want to be re-approving the software. I don't want to be designing the software again" — while explicitly keeping it out of the MVP and forbidding any approach to other clearing houses for now ("We're fine for now"). [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-27-flex-iq-multiple-clearinghouses-selectable-per-device.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Multiple_Clearinghouse_Selection-August-27-2026.md]]
- **He named QA/CI as the single thing that decides whether the NOVA migration is worth anything.** "если мы не сможем достигнуть… то все фишенцы, с которой мы здесь достигли, они равны нулю. Потому что мы можем двигаться только *** asfast слово tard" (Pavel supplied "Боттлмек"); "если мы сейчас решим эту проблему? То мы можем двигаться очень быстро." His method: reverse-engineer requirements and acceptance criteria from the existing prose and code, working back through the overall architecture, and keep the prose in the repository next to the code so Claude can generate and run the tests. See [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Aug-28). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dragon_Pays_Nova_Conversion_Review-August-28-2026.md]]
- **He cut his own theoretical argument short in favour of an experiment.** After Pavel's animation-determinism objection: "давайте сейчас чтото очень много теорий. Давайте мы опустимся в практику… Давайте, чтобы Игорь опустился в это, и он нам скажет, что работает, что не работает." He also named his evidence base for the 70/90/100%-automated-QA claims as engineers he follows "на твиттере и на экс." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dragon_Pays_Nova_Conversion_Review-August-28-2026.md]]
- **He restated the standards-repository rule and the distinction behind it:** anything that emerges must go into the company standards repository, because "мы говорим, что ни девелопер чемуто научился. А что j чемуто научился? Это 2 разные вещи" — a developer learning is not JVL learning; standards previous developers contributed "для Клода становятся прозрачными." He said he will personally review a matrix of which developer created which standards. See [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Aug-28). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dragon_Pays_Nova_Conversion_Review-August-28-2026.md]]
- **He scheduled both follow-ups live on the calls** — the Aug-28 Dragon Pays review during the Aug-26 call ("давайте я сразу запланирую этот митинг, чтобы я не забыл"), and the Sept 4 follow-up during the Aug-28 one; the Sept 4 Teams meeting (9:00–10:00 AM ET) was created the same day. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Dragon_Pays_Nova_Conversion_Review-August-28-2026.md]]
- **He spent the same three days selling in person, not only running meetings.** Two customer visits in two days — [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-narayani-amusements-trade-in-negotiation.md|Aug-26 at Narayani Amusements' office]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-27-halcyon-amusements-recycler-pitch.md|Aug-27 hosting Halcyon Amusements at the JVL office]] — in which he introduced himself as "**JVL's actually second generation of the company**", opened with "what is it that I can do for you to uh **buy from us on a regular basis**", and closed a trade-in deal in the room. The three moves he uses (show up where competitors don't; disagree to the customer's face while conceding his frame; hand him the pen, then bound the number) are on [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Aug-26); the deal is [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-narayani-three-machine-d27-trade-in-pilot.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The peak-hour theory he parked internally on Aug 26 he argued hard to customers on Aug 26 and 27** — with a number he does not use internally: "the RTP is ninety percent versus ninety-five percent. That's **fifty percent difference in net win**… people think that it's five percent." Same day he also set the **$337/year/machine** price for buying machine data back, and told a customer JVL's machines failing to earn "has to be **in setting**." See [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]] and [[wiki/topics/machine-data-repurchase-program.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Recycler_Pitch_and_Lottery_Notice-August-27-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Trade_In_Negotiation-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Aug 28 (~9:50 AM ET) — a walk-in tech-support visit turned into prospecting.** When a visiting operator, "Danette" ([[wiki/people/dannettee-hickman.md|Dannettee Hickman]]), mentioned a route "in a couple of different states", he stopped her: "hold on, hold on, hold on! Don't skip. Over, come on. Tell me more. Which states?… How can how can you make money for me?" He offered her "**refurbished machines**" and an introduction to a woman who "worked for me for **CashCode**… from 2000… to 2006 until I sold it" and now lives in Texas, as a possible partner. This is the first contact in the corpus with the operator he met again on Sept 16. [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-28-danette-texas-operator-office-visit.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Danette_Texas_Operator_Office_Visit-August-28-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear across all six sessions — market, device and programme economics only; no named-person compensation. *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)*

## Aug 31 2026 — selling to a locked-in customer, and committing out loud to a market he has not read the rules for

Two Georgia sessions in one day: a ~1h 41m working meeting with [[wiki/people/georgios-zazanis.md|George Zazanis]] of [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md|ARC Gaming]] ([[wiki/meetings/2026-08-31-arc-gaming-transactional-demo-class-a.md|stub]]), and a 12-minute office exchange that opened a new sales channel ([[wiki/meetings/2026-08-31-c-store-trade-show-go-to-market.md|stub]]).

- **He set the frame against a pitch, twice.** "let us give you a not a presentation… **I wanted it to be an idea exchange**" — and then defined why JVL asks before it sells: "because we're a technology company, we go to large operators and we say, '**How do you do your own business? Tell us.** Do you want bill acceptors in?… We'll do it for you, but we gotta understand how you do [it].'" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He ran the meeting off one question and would not let it go:** "**Everything that I'm telling you, keep one thing in mind: what happens if a location does not pay.**" It took him ten minutes of pressing to get ARC's COO to name his own exposure, and that answer became the whole commercial case. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He deliberately de-emphasised his own hardware.** "**Kiosk business not needed.** I'll show you. We'll issue the card here and we'll play machine. **Treat this device as a printer**," and "I actually **put very little emphasis on selling kiosks**… I will happily deal with you just for cloud services." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He withheld the mechanism on purpose.** "I do everything automatically… **And I'm not going to tell you how because it's my secret.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **Faced with a contract the customer cannot exit, he proposed keeping the shell and swapping the brain** — "Keep your kiosk the way it is. I'll throw away the board inside and put my own board" — and accepted being routed through ARC's CEO rather than approaching the vendor he personally has known for thirty years. Ruling on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-arc-kiosk-board-swap-routed-through-the-ceo.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He read the customer's political position back to him accurately, then told him to be careful.** "I've been in business for many, many years, and **when you say it is what it is, I understand what it means**. When your CEO tells you, George, it is what it is." And: "**Just don't lose your job, George. You ask too many questions.**" He also flagged his own interrupting mid-demo — "Sorry, Andrei, I keep interrupting. I don't mean that. I apologize." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He committed to a Class A deal inside three months** — "George, I will have a deal within three months… January. We're gonna do business" — before reading the Class A regulations, which he asked for in the same conversation and did not receive. Ruling on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-class-a-deal-with-arc-within-three-months.md]]; the market on [[wiki/topics/georgia-class-a-machines.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **He wanted to go and look the same morning.** "George, can you please leave right now? Go to the office. Call me with the number, and **in half an hour I will be there**… I'm gonna be filming… Send me to a friendly location that will allow me to film." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **His reasoning-by-analogue, again.** The case for Class A was Germany: two firms, unlimited licences, limited payout, ~150,000 machines, "**Nobody thought that it would ever make any money.**" Same move he makes elsewhere with casino floor-rating practice, used here to explain why the industry reports flatter small fleets. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Transactional_Demo_and_Class_A-August-31-2026.md]]
- **In the afternoon he approved a channel outside the operator layer in one line.** "**Definitely. Good thinking. Totally with you on this**" — and immediately offered his own contact as the workaround for a sold-out show: "I actually have a friend of mine who's going to that… Maybe I could get a small piece from him. Do you want me to find out?" Ruling on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-convenience-store-trade-shows-as-a-channel.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_C_Store_Trade_Show_Go_To_Market-August-31-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear across both sessions — operator, machine, licence and show economics only; no named-person compensation.

*(The four commitments Val took on this day — the Class A deal, the Class A location visit/film, the ARC kiosk-conversion proposal, and asking his friend about the C-Store Connect booth — are held once on their decision pages and are deliberately not duplicated or re-counted here.)*

## Sept 1–3 2026 — "a standard has one location", the prose-to-tests chain, and a disagreement he conceded

- **Sept 1, morning — the fifth Georgia operator visit in a week** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-double-down-gaming-visit.md|Double Down Gaming at the Lawrenceville office]], 10:25–11:53 AM ET). Three things he does here he does nowhere else in the corpus quite as plainly. **(1) He interrogated the trade association like an auditor** — budget, board size, terms, who is paid, the political champion, the lobbyist, the law firms, and who funds campaigns — then asked for the board-member list "sent to me personally." On [[wiki/organizations/gamoa.md]]. **(2) He made the RTP-parity argument in full**: JVL's settings range is "humongous," generalised settings are the defect, and he will only be compared like-for-like — "Set up your [Light and Wonder] and [Aristocrat] at ninety five percent, and I'll set up this game at ninety five percent. I'll do that." On [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]]. **(3) He warned a prospect about a market failure that hurts JVL's own model** — locations running three validation suppliers against each other and paying none on time — while in the same meeting offering equipment that installs alongside the incumbent, one day after ruling that JVL would only place it exclusively. On [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]]. He also gave his ranking of who must be satisfied: "**you, or location, or legislator, is less important than the player**… a player is the king." No sale; the customer passed. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Double_Down_Gaming_Operations_Review-September-1-2026.md]]
- **Sept 1** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-nova-prose-qa-automation-check.md|prose/QA check-back]], ~19 minutes, called by him as "квыклы… чек бэк"): he ruled that prose is written for the NOVA **platform**, not for the trial game — and noted the team deliberately avoids the word "framework" because "мне объяснили, что это больше, чем [framework]. Я это называю платформой." He restated the chain end to end (prose → architecture → capabilities → requirements with acceptance criteria → automatic end-to-end tests) and named the inversion JVL always makes: "мы, как обычно, как всегда, мы делаем сначала код." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_QA_Automation_Strategy-September-1-2026.md]]
- **His QA line hardened into a ruling.** He recommended freezing the existing hand-written tests and putting Claude's tests in a new warehouse, expecting the old ones to "умрут"; he declined Alexander Biratko's overlap-analysis alternative — "я не вижу связки между старым и то, что новое начинается" — and then closed the topic himself as hypothetical: "это гипотезы[;] жизн[ь] подскаж[е]т, как правильно делать." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-01-freeze-existing-manual-test-cases.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_QA_Automation_Strategy-September-1-2026.md]]
- **Sept 3 — his sharpest process ruling of the month** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-03-nova-prose-standard-skills-and-module-contracts.md|prose/contracts follow-up]]). On learning the new prose standard was sitting in the LGS repository: "Я категорически против таких вещей"; "просто всеми фибрами своей души меня это раздражает"; and the rule itself — a standard, however unfinished, "может быть только единственный [location]… он не может гдето лежать в стороне сбоку сзади в другом репозитории[,] мне удобно, мне неудобно." He apologised for the tone while keeping the point ("Я извиняюсь, если я разговариваю немножко раздражённо, но я действительно раздражён этим походом") and named it as a standing pattern: "он постоянный, я никак его не могу поломать." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-03-prose-standard-into-knowledge-repository.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **He answered Evgeny's "why prose at all" with the business order**, then made his administrative-direction move on capability granularity — telling Claude how JVL works administratively rather than arguing architecture — and was explicit that it was Claude, not he, who wrote his prose: "И не я это писал, а клод[.] давайте правильно будем называть вещи своими именами." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-03-capabilities-consolidated-by-administrative-direction.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **He conceded the acceptance-criteria argument to Irina and bought an experiment.** "Я наверно приду к тому, что у меня нету практических знаний[.] давайте попробуем" — take a piece of code, its prose, capabilities and existing requirements (coin-in, a counter) and see how Claude writes the test. He also questioned his own earlier framing of the task for [[wiki/people/igor-metelski.md|Igor Metelski]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-03-acceptance-criteria-settled-by-experiment.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **He refused the "developers will comply because the repository forces them" framing.** "Я не верю, если [девелоперы] не хотят… людей не заставишь"; someone who simply will not do it "просто не вписывается в коллектив." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **He put the Oct-1 target on the record and asked to be told it is real:** code, prose, capabilities and requirements for the NOVA system modules 90% done by October 1 — "Пожалуйста, скажите, что… это да, потому что иначе… все мои планы [рухнут]" — accepting 10–40% on tests specifically after Pavel's pushback, while suspecting it may all go far faster: "или пойдёт очень, очень быстро… В течение там недели. Или мы чтото неправильно делаем?" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **He cut the theorising and distributed the work.** "Давайте людям раздадим работу, иначе мы потеряем, просто потеряем время"; he expanded the Sept 4 meeting live to include Vladimir, Evgeny, a "Dima" and Anton, left Pavel the task assignment, and set the next check-in a week out, Friday at 08:00. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Nova_Prose_Standard_Skills_Module_Contracts-September-3-2026.md]]
- **Sept 1, afternoon — two more customer visits, and the day's most revealing thirty-five minutes.** After Double Down he ran [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-narayani-akshar-mega-hit-demo-and-trade-in.md|Narayani/Akshar at ~1:05 PM]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-01-air-gaming-group-transactional-walkthrough.md|Air Gaming Group at ~3:00 PM]] — three operator meetings in one day, none of which produced an order. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]
  - **He negotiated under a real clock and said so.** "**I have a secondary market for them**… **It will cost me roughly a thousand dollars per game to recondition it. Now I have secondary market. Tomorrow I don't know**," and "**We're discussing a deal in a different state**… **That's why I'm rushing you to make that deal. I get it. Otherwise I will take somebody else's deal.**" He also restated the Aug-26 trade-in as unsettled — "**you asked for five thousand and I agreed to three thousand**" — which does not match what the Aug-26 record says he agreed. See the open contradiction on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-narayani-three-machine-d27-trade-in-pilot.md]].
  - **He gave advice against his own product interest, twice in one afternoon:** wait for the Georgia Lottery and its counsel before buying cash-dispensing — "**I suggest you wait until GLC and the GLC lawyer rule on the regulation for cash dispensing. So I suggest you don't do it. You wait**" — and told Air Gaming that for bare Georgia compliance "**you don't need any of these units.**" On [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 1).
  - **He put his name behind a guarantee.** "**I can actually give you a guarantee that people cannot copy tickets**… **I guarantee to you, no duplication. I can sign my name on** [it]… **I will do an experiment in Texas with you if you want.**" On [[wiki/organizations/air-gaming-group.md]] and [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]].
  - **And he misjudged a room.** Answering a data-privacy objection he used a crude sexual analogy about losing one's virginity to argue the operator's information was already out; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei]] noted in the room that the visitor "**didn't like it**," and the derived notes flag it for him ahead of the office meeting.
- **Sept 1, after the customers left — 35 minutes of sales coaching, and the argument it started.** The fullest record in the corpus of how Val thinks selling should be done, delivered to [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]] and Andrei: no phone calls in front of a customer, never "no, but", volume repels, posture is a tell, "**Start from focusing on a gem, on something that is a killer. Stick with it. Less is more**," never ask "do you have any questions?", and lead with the single differentiator — the Steve Jobs "**ten thousand tunes in your palm**" argument against BlackBerry's security. He also stated the evidential rule Felipe would not accept: "**If there is one line of garbage, the whole report is dismissed.**" And he invited the disagreement: "**By all means, contradict. There's nothing. I don't get offended by you contradicting me**," conceding one point outright — "**You're absolutely right. It doesn't explain the game.**" Full doctrine on [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear across all these sessions — process/platform content, machine and device pricing, trade-in and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 2 2026 — business pillars for the transactional device family, and "money does not rest"

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-flex-iq-pre-pilot-updates-after-the-show.md|Sept-2 Flex-IQ pre-pilot call]] (Russian) Val took the same posture he had taken on Flex-Port-Mini in August: state the business constraints, refuse to design. "Я бы не хотел бы предлагать инженерные решения… Я бы хотел бы только обсудить [use ca]s[es]. И некоторые… [pillars] o[f] [the] бизнес[а]" — and when he slipped, he caught himself: "Я предложил решение инженерное, то, что я пытаюсь не делать." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]

- **He opened by parking the biggest question rather than debating it.** JVL will not and cannot work with Mutha Goose long-term — "вообще это даже неправильный был подход" — and Flex-Port must run its own wiring to the machines; but "я не хотел бы сейчас… делать это целью этого разговора… Просто чтобы вы держали это в курсе." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **His pillars:** minimise unit cost, because the hardware will most likely be given away free or subsidised "из нашего кармана"; a hard requirement to hold remote access so JVL can disconnect and freeze a location's system if it stops paying; and a flexible topology — the host at the centre, a wide range of attached local devices (wide port, budget port, a ~$9 in-machine SMIB, Flex-IQ, Flex-Pay slim, dashboards, mobile access) whose number and mix must not be rigid. Detail on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept-2). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **He forced the team to state what it actually knows.** "Смартфон это хорошее решение. Но… никто из нас сегодня не знает, это будет [accept]ед игроком или нет[.] Но нам нужно нача[ть] идти кудато" — the one thing JVL can grasp with its own hands today, right or wrong, is printing tickets; "Куда дальше это пойдет. Пока непонятно." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **He attacked the premise of the barcode objection rather than its detail** — "почему вы так пришли к этому анализу?" and "мы вообще к этому ничего общего не имеем" — which let Andrei close it in two sentences. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-activate-incomm-cards-by-barcode-on-flex-iq.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **His method on the firmware-update question:** stop guessing frequency, work out what could need updating and the frequency follows — "Если мы знаем, что конкретно можно [апдейтить], тогда можно себе представить, как часто это, скорее всего, возникнет." His cost read: 2 MB to 4 MB of flash is insignificant money. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-flex-port-sas-universal-with-scanner-touch-vs-non-touch.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **He set the priorities and the market:** IQ, ports, Flex-Pay and immediately the Flex recycler, with Texas enterable today; wallets, not TITO, is the scheme JVL goes in with. He separated his forecast from the near-term fact on validators — operators "снимут в конце концов[.] Но это моё личное мнение… на последующие там год или 2 они не будут сниматься." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-priorities-iq-ports-flex-pay-recycler-texas-on-wallets.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **He overrode the Friday follow-up to Monday and dismissed the public holiday:** "Выходных дней Андрей у нас не бывает, так как и отпусков. Потому что деньги не отдыхают." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **He checked back at the very close** that the three items Andrei raised at the start would be done that week — "я не понял… вот на 3 пункта… я понял, что это на этой неделе будет сделано" — and made Pavel restate them. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — device, board, memory and market economics only; no named-person compensation. **§15 #9:** clear.

## Sept 2 2026 (in Georgia) — a customer's complaint taken straight, his credentials on the table before a refusal, and a sale he declined to chase

The same day as the Russian-language pre-pilot call above, he ran four meetings in Georgia: [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-lucky-coin-service-and-upgrade-offer.md|Lucky Coin at 9:29 AM]], the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-incomm-machine-payments-and-recycler-standoff.md|InComm working lunch from ~11:55]], the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-continental-bifurcated-operations-and-vendor-lock-in.md|Continental visit at ~3:16 PM]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-billy-liu-playpay-partnership-deep-dive.md|the PlayPay deep-dive at ~6:14 PM]].

- **He asked the question most sellers avoid, and did not defend the answer.** "What is holding you back from buying JVL as a primary supplier? **Tell me the truth**" — then, when the room drifted into whose fault the outages were, he stopped it: "**I don't think that our machines should be breaking. Period**… it should be **my problem**… if it broke, **I should be the first one to know**." He would not give a date — "**I will fix it. I just cannot commit to a date**" — and named what he offered instead as a mask, not a fix. Full doctrine on [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Sept 2); the complaint on [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **He bought his way into an account rather than discounting into it.** "**Today I am buying my way in**… I'm looking for operators that **could put up with me while I'm developing things**" — six ports and a terminal free per location against a $15,750 machine, at $90/month. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **At lunch he led with forty years of credentials before describing the product that was about to be refused.** The fullest account of his industry history in the corpus, given unprompted to InComm [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]]:
  - "**in nineteen ninety, I started a company which was called CashCode**… a technology company startup which eventually grew into a company that **I sold to CPI**… **CPI bought me, and we served as a technology springboard for CPI to purchase everybody else. The latest purchase for CPI was MEI, which used to be Mars Electronics**, which was [a] subsidiary of M and M Candies." On what that business became: "**CPI is even printing money for 26 different jurisdictions.**"
  - "**I also sat on [the] board of directors. Was reelected for six years, from two thousand to two thousand and six, in Vegas**… **GSA**… **Gaming Standards Association**. So **I was the guy who wrote standards for system to system, peripheral devices to machines** — the protocols… **We were the source for those standards.**" *(The corpus already carried "six terms on the Gaming Standards Association board" from a Feb-2026 meeting bio; this source gives the years. Recorded together, not reconciled into a single claim.)*
  - "**The killer application for CashCode was bill-to-bill**… a **bill hopper**. A hopper means something that old slot machines used to have — this bucket that would turn and spit out coins… **our goal was to design a bill hopper. Hence, bill to bill. This is when we start thinking and developing the bill dispensing format.**" His conclusion, which is the whole point of telling it: "**it's not that I just stepped into it. I understand the bill dispensing format and the cost associated with it quite intimately.**"
  - And a first in the corpus: "**we developed self-checkout counters. There was a company in Montreal. We supplied them with the first technology**… **in the 90s, we were pioneers of this. Nobody had it.**"
  - At Lucky Coin the same morning he gave the earlier half of it: "I started my career **repairing mechanical jukeboxes and mechanical pinball machines** in locations… **at the age of seventeen, my father and I we started this business**, and I was the guy who was repairing mechanical relay machines" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **He accepted a commercial constraint he does not accept intellectually, and refused to sign anything about it.** "it **leaves me with no choice but not to do it**" — and, to the request for a written undertaking, "**I won't say that**… you should not ask me to guarantee something else… **consult your legal counsel because I have one in house too**." Then he closed it warmly: "**I am more of a lover than a fighter**… we should **work towards the business instead of shutting down the business**." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-hold-the-recycler-back-but-sign-no-undertaking.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **He stated JVL's weakest point in Georgia out loud, to an outside party.** "we have many **service-level relationships, but we don't have any top-level relationships**… **like none, zero**," and "**there's nobody here for JVL that can do it. I don't have a senior-level management that can build relationships**" — "very problematic for us. **Very concerning for me.**" On [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Sept 2) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **His Georgia presence, as of this week.** "**My business is here**" [in Georgia]; he is "**here weekly now**." *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]]
- **A third meeting the same afternoon — and he told a customer to his face that there was no business.** At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-continental-bifurcated-operations-and-vendor-lock-in.md|Continental visit]] (~3:16 PM) he heard out an operator locked into Aristocrat and Access and then said it plainly: "**Doesn't look like we can do business here.** No. Well, I mean, I don't know. **It seems like your mind is set on aristocrat and access system.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]
- **And he priced accordingly, and explained why rather than hiding it.** Quoting $1,000 per machine plus $1,000 for the POS plus $90/month per location against an Access install the customer had bought for $5,000 across nine machines: "**Access gave you a wholesale price. I'm giving you a retail price. I'm giving you retail because you're not my customer.** But of course, **when push comes to shove, and you would like to buy, we can sit down and talk.**" The same afternoon he also praised the competitor without qualification — "**Novomatic is a very good company. I love Novomatic.**" It is the counterpart to the Lucky Coin morning: there he bought his way into an account; here he declined to. Detail on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 2, Continental). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]
- **In the car afterwards he supplied the commercial read of the day**, that the operator's replay request is really about the Intralot reporting gap and amounts to "**a wonderful way for location to keep on giving cash and skirting everything under**" — and, when [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei]] drew out that this means card volumes will not rise, he conceded he had no counter: "**I'm just not sure I have a reply.**" On [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 2, Continental). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]
- **A fourth meeting that evening, and he ended it by trying to buy half a company.** At [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-billy-liu-playpay-partnership-deep-dive.md|the PlayPay deep-dive]] (~6:14 PM) he spent most of an hour refusing to let a founder sell him anything — "**You don't need to sell me on anything. I'm trying to understand. I'm being technology agnostic**… **Let's remain neutral**" — and then, once he had the fee stack out of him, proposed a partnership on the spot: "**You want me to write a contract? Why don't you sleep on it?… I get fifty percent of your company. I'm driving you right into the market.**" Terms, arithmetic and the two disagreements are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-val-proposes-fifty-percent-of-play-payments.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The method is visible in that meeting more clearly than anywhere else: he interrogates until the number falls out.** He re-ran the same player journey five or six times — "**So I'm gonna do it again. I come in as a player**" — refusing summaries and pushing past deflections ("**Billy, how do you make money?… So if you transacted a billion dollars, how much money would you make? This is quantitative physics**"). The counterparty's verdict, given mid-argument: "**Val, you're the brightest person that I've met in the gaming business because you calculate every goddamn thing**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].
- **And he named the thing he thinks is actually scarce.** "**There is no magic in this solution. The magic is in speed.** That's why I'm meeting with you today… **Tomorrow. Everyone will have this and this. Tomorrow the game is over.**" It is the same instinct as the morning's "buying my way in," argued from time rather than price. On [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 2, evening) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].
- **§15.8:** clear across all four meetings — machine, device, card, equity and market economics only; no compensation figure for any named person.

## Sept 4 2026 — two meetings, one instruction: the same standards rule to engineering and to the game team

Val ran both halves of the company through the same point on one day — [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-rage-nova-conversion-readiness.md|9:04 AM in Russian with engineering]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-naming-convention-standards-and-nova-conversion.md|2:07 PM in English with the game team]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Rage_Nova_Conversion_Readiness-September-4-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]].

- **The standing instruction, given twice:** use the standards, add to them, and point your Claude at the standards repository first. Access has been granted and manually verified for all **39 accounts**. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-point-claude-at-the-standards-repository-first.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Nova_Conversion_Readiness-September-4-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He closed the data-contracts argument by authority, not by answer** — "Я уважаю ваши сомнения… Я на 90% уверен, что все эти вопросы уйдут… Давайте это оставим как… внутренние разговоры, как это сделать? **Не меняя систему**" — and set September 11 as the date for every repository to carry its contract, asking who would not do it and getting no hands. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-data-contracts-closed-every-repository-by-september-11.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Nova_Conversion_Readiness-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He stated the development pipeline end to end for the first time** (product owner → capabilities → requirements with acceptance criteria → features → feature branches → code + prose → AI programming and end-to-end testing) and asked for disagreement; nobody disagreed. His premise: "через год это будет совершенно на другом уровне. А через 3 года наверно это достигнет суп[еринтеллидженс]. А если этому верить? То хотелось бы наладить [pipeline]." And his own positioning: "я не инженер, **я администратор**… Пожалуйста, поправьте меня." See [[wiki/topics/nova-platform-architecture.md]] and [[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]] (Sept-4). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Nova_Conversion_Readiness-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He named the politeness problem in his own engineering team.** "У нас у всех есть какой[-]то… Элемент вежливости, у меня его нету… вы боитесь друг друга обидеть" — using Anton's Test 360 work as the example nobody would criticise out of kindness, and calling his own approach "брутальная… честность." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Nova_Conversion_Readiness-September-4-2026.md]]
- **In the afternoon he lost the same argument he won in the morning,** and said so. Against Lena's "it was verbal agreement" he made his standing case — "The standards are always agreed verbally. It becomes tribal knowledge… I have been trying to root it out for years" — then conceded the room: "It seems like there is absolutely zero chance. I stand 0 chance of proving my point… 100% disagreed with you. Ridiculous statements… I choose not to continue. Let's move on." He returned to the point calmly twice more. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-asset-naming-convention-must-be-written-down.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He reconstructed the Georgia V3 slip by inference and made the team confirm it** — "I'm reading between the lines here… don't kill me" — landing on the Queen of Luxor graphics decision rather than bugs, and establishing he had been part of the decision though the September 10 date itself was never stated. See [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Sept-4). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He argued against his own taste on the V-deck.** "I love VDEC… But I've learned to understand that whatever I love is not necessarily good"; "we don't have to satisfy Val. We have to make money." And he repeatedly flagged the theory as untested: "I'm making a theory out of it, but it might be total BS." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-georgia-v4-mechanical-button-deck.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **He put his Georgia market read on the record in full** — 47,000 machines in 7,400 locations, the status-symbol dynamics of the C-store channel, and "Sam doesn't buy machines. It's the operator that buys machines, and the operator will buy whatever Sam wants" — as the argument for a premium Curve cabinet. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-d27-discontinued-curve-c43-premium-line.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **And he described his own pricing improvisation at the show**, step by step: the giveaway package, the rise to $14,500, the 15-game / $15,750 construction that "kind of flew out of my mouth," and fencing the package to the higher price. See [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] (Sept-4). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]
- **On the regulator, he declined to treat Eclipse as a safety net:** "Eclipse doesn't test anything. Eclipse only tests us… They're testing SAS. So it's nothing" — and answered Juan's GLI-timing question with readiness rather than a date. **No NOVA certification date was set.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Naming_Convention_Standards_and_Nova_Conversion-September-4-2026.md]]

## Sept 4 2026 (midday) — the two calls between the morning and the afternoon meetings

Between the 9:04 AM engineering meeting and the 2:07 PM game-team meeting above, Val ran two back-to-back hardware calls: the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-lte-modem-firmware-mismatch-and-kore-settings.md|12:10–12:29 modem call]] and the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-flex-slim-first-ten-and-flex-port-sas.md|12:29–1:49 production call]].

- **He pressed an accountability argument on [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] and it did not land.** "Вы заблуждаетесь в том, что вы проверили[,] всё работало[,] а сейчас не работает. Вот этот вот концепт, сам по себе он ошибочный" — with the seniority frame ("если бы вам было сейчас 25 лет… но вам помоему уже не 25"). Full argument on [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Sept 4). He read the outcome honestly to Genadi minutes later: "Я пытался Павла провести, он помоему был в шоке от моего комментария и уехал, расстроенный домой. Поэтому оставим его, чтоб он отдохнул, и вернёмся к этому в понедельник" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].
- **He refused to assert the timing from memory.** Having said he raised the modem "давнымдавно," he stopped himself: "я не не хочу просто говорить голословно, но я проверю, когда я это начал просить… у меня столько мессед[жей] на эту тему… После этого разговора я проверю" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **He redirected the modem problem to the vendor rather than to more guessing:** "Давайте мы спросим [KORE] какие модемы, они рекомендуют" — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-north-america-modem-variant-and-ask-kore-for-the-apn.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_LTE_Modem_Firmware_Mismatch_and_KORE_Settings-September-4-2026.md]].
- **He explained his own commercial reasoning to his hardware lead**, unprompted, as a way of making the schedule pressure legible: "я хочу вам объяснить, что я делаю, потому что если вы поймёте, что я делаю[,] вам легче будет думать," then "Вы понимаете, что я вас не просто так толкаю?" The arithmetic is on [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] (Sept 4, midday), and his stated objective was "мы в принципе должны убить рынок этим оф[ф]ром" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].
- **He killed two proposals in one call** — the screenless Flex-Port ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-flex-port-keeps-its-screen.md]]) and the local bracket shop ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-brackets-and-harnesses-from-china-and-a-metal-order-for-one-hundred.md]]) — and cancelled the long weekend: "Нету праздников[,] выходных[,] уикендов. Это я всё отменил" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].
- **He talked himself out of over-spec'ing the metal**, taking Genadi's "14 ге[й]ч это годы наф" with "Да. Согласен" — and explained why he had asked for new bill acceptors and printers in the first place: "я хотел посчитать правильную себестоимость" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

## Sept 7 2026 — three meetings in one morning: he condemns MudaGoose, opens the Flex-Ports to Wi-Fi, closes the old-machine market, and takes work off a person

The morning opened with a call that was **not** his — Andrei Klimovich's [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-gacs-show-setup-flex-iq-flex-ports-and-the-e-wallet.md|8:00 GACS show-setup call]] — after which he organised and ran the other two ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-wifi-on-flex-port-and-qr-provisioning.md|9:08 Wi-Fi]], [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-sas-aft-on-flex-port-and-remote-update.md|10:02 SAS/AFT]], all Russian), closing the last promising to send the day's transcripts to the participants.

### 8:00 — the reversal, and the plainest admission of error in the corpus

- **He told the team he had misled them, and named the reason as business, not engineering.** Having said earlier on the same call that Flex-IQ and MudaGoose "скорее всего, будут существовать на одном и том же лак[ейшне] параллельно," he cut Klimovich off fifteen minutes later: "мы должны[,] мы обязаны [cannibalise] [Муда]гус… **Я ошибочно ввёл вас в заблуждение**, когда я думал, что… эта схема может жить параллельно. Это не технологическая вещь, это бизнес[-]вещь." Asked point-blank by Chudinov whether combined installations would happen at all: "Нет, не будет." Ruling on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-iq-cannibalises-mudagoose-no-parallel-operation.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He imported a meeting method by name.** Nobody had read the Confluence page, so: "Шеф Безос… заставляет всех читать и обсуждать вещи прямо на митинге. Считаю, что ни у кого не было возможности [про]читать[ся]" — read and argue it here, now [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He refused to let the stand be treated as a stand.** "мы сейчас не просто говорим, что вот у нас будет трейд[-шоу]… Мы сейчас активно занимаемся продажей… определённых решений в Джорджии… это не только для трейд[-шоу]… и для самого рынка." Then he pushed past the show entirely: "я работаю сильно над тем, чтобы сделать installation **в сентябре**… **Мне нужен продукт сейчас**… тот, кто захватит сегодня вот этот рынок [первым,] тот будет им контролировать… сегодня нужно работать в системе **over[-]драйв**… Мне нужн[ы] IQ [и] flex pay. **Завтра**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He argued a position for twenty minutes and then declined to rule on it.** Against allowing play to start without a wallet: "Если вы разрешите это делать, то это будет так всегда, а если вы один раз не разрешите, то вы всегда научите лок[ации], как правильно делать. **Я не настаиваю ни на одном, ни на другом, потому что я не знаю, как сделать правильно.**" He accepted Klimovich's market argument as serious — "В этом есть серьёзная мысль" — and left it to the field: "это жизнь подскажет." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-the-e-wallet-must-exist-with-a-zero-balance.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He deferred an architecture decision on grounds of attention, not merit.** On moving wallets to the cloud: "Загружать сейчас свой мозг этим — это мне кажется[,] просто выставка… Шансы очень, очень маленькие… И инсталляций будет не очень много" — six months to JVL's own cloud server, everything moves then. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-e-wallets-stay-on-the-local-gateway-until-jvl-has-its-own-cloud.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He fixed the product name mid-sentence and asked for the document to be changed.** "не слым пэй, а **пей слэм**… Поменяйте, пожалуйста" — a Flex-Pay slim configuration, as the derived notes read it [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **His tolerance for ugly-but-shipped, stated as a market read.** Told a competitor had put a badly 3D-printed case straight into production: "Все торопятся сегодня покорить рынок, кто сделает… тот будет контролировать" (the middle of the sentence is garbled beyond reconstruction) — and to Genadi, "сделайте простой[,] самый тупой [enclosure] — и едем дальше." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-one-tito-printer-and-one-paper-for-everything.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]

*(His one commitment from this meeting — to hold a separate session on the Flex recycler — is held once on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] and deliberately not re-counted here.)*

### 9:08 and 10:02

- **He reversed himself twice in one morning, out loud.** Having ruled Wi-Fi out on Aug 24, he opened the 9:08 call asking for exactly that. Then, mid-meeting, he proposed making a JVL device the access point to save the router cost and immediately withdrew it: "мы не будем исключать[ся], я дурную идею дал… Я просто потупому сказал, без мозгов." Both are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-module-on-flex-port-with-an-external-router.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He would not take an engineer's assurance on antennas:** "со своей антенной не пойдет, это никогда не работает. Я уже натыкался на эти грабли… потому что меня какой-то инженер [уверял], что это работает" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **His standing requirement, stated as culture:** every device with a display or lamps shows what it sees — "это культура, это культура" — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-every-jvl-device-must-show-what-it-sees.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **Geopolitics as a procurement rule:** "нам нужно американские роутеры, потому что китайские будут запрещены в Америке через некоторое время." Nokia and MikroTik passed [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **"If it is not written up in Jira, there is no work"** — and he refused, five times over, to let the existing ticket simply be amended [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He drew the commercial line on SAS himself:** "проект флекс[-]порт сда[ё]тся с[о] [SAS]. Мы уходим от этого гибкого подключения, и мы не будем адресовать ту часть рынка, которая сегодня стоит на старых машинах" — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-port-goes-sas-and-jvl-drops-the-old-machine-segment.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He named his own priority as robustness, not features:** "мне лучше меньше функционала добавить, но все эти эр[рор ре]ко[в]ери [и] сервис ит[е]мс мне нужно впереди" — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-error-recovery-and-non-functional-requirements-come-first.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He took a task off a named person and said why.** On remote firmware update: "Давайте мы Яворского снимем, потому что [с] Яворским мне очень тяжело работать… я бы не хотел на Яворского иметь какие-то таски" — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-remote-firmware-update-mandatory-and-taken-off-yavorsky.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- **His time pressure, verbatim:** "Мне нужно это завтра" (9:08), and to Bosik, "поскольку мне нужно это завтра, то вы вчера должны были начать это делать" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He flagged his own joke for the transcript.** After blaming Musk for the memory price spike: "но для транскрипта это просто шутка" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He asked to be reminded.** On the MudaGoose interim question he deferred a day or two — "только напомните мне, я забыл" — and said he would be on site the next day [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]

### 11:02 — the fourth meeting, and the only non-engineering one

Straight out of the SAS call he took an hour with **[[wiki/people/vitaly-timokhov.md|Vitaly Timokhov]]**, outside tax counsel ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-departure-tax-trust-and-bad-debt.md]]), opening it: "Let's make it efficient because I gotta jump on the plane." *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)* What belongs here: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Departure_Tax_Trust_and_Bad_Debt-September-7-2026.md]]

- **He now has three JVL corporate projects running through outside tax counsel** — preserve [[wiki/organizations/jvl-labs.md|JVL Labs]]' CCPC status and the ~$1M a year in tax credits, utilise the $26M 680→JVL loan as a bad debt, and possibly move the US subsidiaries to [[wiki/people/inna-levitan.md|Inna]] personally. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Departure_Tax_Trust_and_Bad_Debt-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Departure_Tax_Trust_and_Bad_Debt-September-7-2026.md]]
- **He corrected his own lawyer on the shareholding.** Vitaly assumed Inna was a director and Val the shareholder; Val: "**Inna is the owner.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Departure_Tax_Trust_and_Bad_Debt-September-7-2026.md]]
- **Same impatience as the engineering calls, applied to professionals.** Told Vitaly would phone [[wiki/people/sean-riley.md|Sean Riley]] "tomorrow," Val pushed back — "I would prefer for you to **set up a call today** if possible, for tomorrow" — and Vitaly agreed to email immediately. He also asked to hear back "by the end of the week." And when Vitaly's explanation wandered: "I honestly am confused. **Can you say what you want to say in the most focused fashion?**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Departure_Tax_Trust_and_Bad_Debt-September-7-2026.md]]

*(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)*

## Sept 8 2026 (Bank of America call) — declaring himself out of the room, and telling the bank his own numbers are wrong

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-08-bank-of-america-cashpro-api-capabilities.md|Sept-8 Bank of America CashPro call]] (12:30–1:05 PM ET, with [[wiki/people/nathan-carver.md|Nathan Carver]]) Val did something he does rarely and did explicitly here: he announced his own irrelevance to the subject matter and then held to it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Bank_of_America_CashPro_API_Integration-September-8-2026.md]]

- **He put his role on the record at the top.** "I am just an executive standing by, just in case you need executive decisions. I'm not an engineer. I will not be participating in anything, so I'll be silent in the meeting. I'm here only for executive purposes." He then spoke only four more times on substance — the China suppliers, the wire volumes, "there is no urgency," and "that works." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Bank_of_America_CashPro_API_Integration-September-8-2026.md]]
- **He warned the bank off his own figures before the bank could rely on them.** Asked for wire volumes he said roughly **$150,000 US a month over about ten wires**; when Brooks flagged that his file showed twenty-five to thirty a month, Val explained "It includes domestic. I was focused on China specific," then discredited himself unprompted: "my numbers are all over the place. I never really sit down and calculate it. I'm really approximating and shooting from my hips. So these numbers are incorrect… It's a placeholder." The bank is nonetheless building the pricing proposal on them. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Bank_of_America_CashPro_API_Integration-September-8-2026.md]]
- **He declined to create urgency.** Brooks apologised that the pricing proposal might slip to the next day because he was "literally back to back till five thirty"; Val: "There is no urgency. Tomorrow is fine." Approving that proposal is what starts a five-to-seven-week implementation. Full substance on [[wiki/organizations/bank-of-america.md]] (Sept 8). §15.8 clear. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Bank_of_America_CashPro_API_Integration-September-8-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Bank_of_America_CashPro_API_Integration-September-8-2026.md]]
