## Sept 9 2026 — two meetings ninety minutes apart: the commercial window, the mobilisation, and one argument he could not win

Wednesday morning: a Russian call with Minsk 7:02–8:38 AM ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-september-resource-plan-and-flex-port-readiness.md]]), then an English in-person meeting at 8:43 ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-october-rollout-staging-and-unit-ramp.md]]). He carried the same agenda into both — October 1, one unit per location, SAS, negative cases, Wi-Fi later — and the substance sits on the Sept-9 decision pages.

**Why he is pushing.** The clearest statement in the corpus of the window he believes JVL is inside: JVL is "[е]динственная компания, которая выпускает машину игральную и транзак[шнл] сами"; "[т]олько что рынок открылся на транзакционный [эквипмент]… это долго длиться не будет… [в] один день выключат[,] выключат свет… клик[,] всё остановится"; and therefore "мне с сегодняшнего дня до декабря нужно **мобилизировать людей по максимуму**… И в декабре мы сможем отдыхать[,] в конце декабря. Но сейчас мне нужно **больше, чем максимум**[ —] мне нужны вещи, которые мы никогда не могли сделать." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]] Full version on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-the-ramp-counts-units-not-locations.md]].

**What he promised in exchange.** "[М]не надо, чтобы люди работали овер[]тайм… не за[]бесплатно[.] **Я всем время скомпенсирую**"; "Я за всё заплачу всем. Никто в обиде не останется." The derived notes read the same passage as a commitment to compensate all overtime for the push from now to December. *(Recorded as a stated commitment; §15.8 — no rate, sum or individual's pay appears in the source.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]

**And the stakes as he put them:** "[я] не могу сделать ошибку[ —] очень много денег… Я абсолютно не могу позволить никакой ошибки. Всё должно быть обдуманно и сделано гладко." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]

**The argument he stopped rather than won.** He challenged the whole practice of writing requirements before testing — "[т]огда зачем все пишут? Почему все говорят, что надо писать? Все[,] значит[,] не правы?" — got the automation-cost answer from Irina and the long-horizon answer from Pavel, and parked it: "[н]у хорошо, давайте мы пока запаркуем[;] это я должен подумать." Full exchange on [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 9). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]

**His stated view of how to prompt Claude**, offered while reading requirement 1404 on the call: "[К]лоду лучше давать **general**, чем **definite**… чем больше ты даёшь [Клоду] дефини[ций,] тем меньше он употребляет свой [imagination]." Recorded as his view, not as a property of the tool. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]

**Stated on the call:** "[…] я работаю сейчас приблизительно **на 30% больше**, чем я работал раньше," alongside his general position that "люди, которые не работают круглосуточно[ —] с ними что[-]то не в порядке." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]

**In the second meeting** he did three things worth keeping on this page: he refused to take the commercial question at all ("I respect your question, but I would like to handle your question **not in the scope of this meeting**"), he named the contractual instrument he wants at every location (exclusive agreement plus a **non-disturbance clause** — "when it comes to enforcement, I want to have all the aces in my deck"), and he gave a hiring instruction by trade rather than by title (**junior electrical tradesmen with an electrical certificate**, not technicians or programmers). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_October_Rollout_Staging_and_Unit_Ramp-September-9-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_October_Rollout_Staging_and_Unit_Ramp-September-9-2026.md]] He also noted "today is my last day. Tomorrow I have a bunch of appointments on the road. You won't see me." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_October_Rollout_Staging_and_Unit_Ramp-September-9-2026.md]]

## Sept 10 2026 — alone, with breakfast, in front of the regulator

The "appointments on the road" he announced on Sept 9 turn out to include the one meeting he had never had: an introduction to the [[wiki/organizations/georgia-lottery-corporation.md|Georgia Lottery]] COAM staff in their own hearing room in Atlanta, 10:21–11:20 AM ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-10-georgia-lottery-coam-manufacturer-introduction.md]]). He went **alone**, brought breakfast, and described his own method in the room: "**I am just very, very informal individual. Like I come by myself and with breakfast, and that's… how I function. I'm trying to avoid any confusion. Legislation… is expensive. Litigation is expensive. I'm trying to go fishing.**" He left that evening — "**I'm leaving tonight**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

**Two questions, asked plainly, and his refusal to accept indirection.** He asked whether building Class A would make enemies, and whether JVL's transactional equipment is legal under the prize-card rules. On the demonstration venue he would not take a hint: "**I… don't read between the lines. I… need a binary answer.**" Outcomes on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-the-lottery-tells-jvl-class-a-manufacturing-is-lawful-today.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-no-class-a-build-until-the-january-legislature-is-heard.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-the-transactional-equipment-demonstration-goes-to-intralot.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

**The posture he took with a regulator**, which is characteristic and worth keeping: he disclaimed politics entirely — "**I'm not a politician, and I'm not here for politics. I'm… a money-making machine**… **Is it legal or not?**" — while insisting on going first rather than apologising later: "**What I don't like to do is to discuss it post-fact. I think that's just bad manners**", and "**I just don't want to be coming into your backyard and shitting all over the place.**" He also accepted that their answer has a shelf life: "**I am not relying on you for legislation**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

**He conceded, to the regulator, that the August show demonstration may have gone too far:** "**I understand that what I showed… could have been misunderstood, or I've created too much excitement. The goal is to tame it down and to bring it into… legislative compliance format**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 10).

**Two cost signals dropped in passing:** he is not attending G2E — asked directly, "**I can't afford it**" — and he prefers bringing equipment to Atlanta because "**Much cheaper for me to bring it here and set it up for you here**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]].

### The biography he gave in the room (Sept 10 2026)

The fullest single telling in the corpus of his own origin story, and it adds dates the earlier accounts lack. Recorded as self-description [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Lottery_Class_A_Guidance_and_Prize_Card_Compliance-September-10-2026.md]]:

- *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)*
- **The education, and his father's ultimatum.** "**I went to got degree in digital, sorry, diploma in digital electronics**… **my father said, 'Decide quickly: digital electronics or accounting.'**" And the punchline he tells against himself: "**that profession was gone. I think within five years, nobody needed digital electronics anymore. It was all embedded.**"
- **Arcade games were the fallback, not the plan.** "**Well, I couldn't find a job, so I started a business, and the only business was available at that time was coin-operated games**"; his favourite is "**Miss Pac-Man, to be… technically correct**".
- **The bill-acceptor deal — a version this page did not have.** "**in 1990, I had this customer. He was an engineer… 'I have an idea of building a bill acceptor. I want you to become my fifty percent partner.' Said no… that's not for me. So he went to my father, spoke to my father, and my father loved the idea**… **so we became partners in this company. We became uber, uber, uber successful, and we sold that company in 2006 to CPI.**" *(Val's Sept-2 2026 telling on this page says "in nineteen ninety, I started a company which was called CashCode". The two are compatible — he ends up a partner either way — but the Sept-10 version has him initially declining. Recorded side by side, not merged.)* He added: "**CPI later bought Mai, which was Marshall Economy**" — garbled and not interpretable; the Sept-2 telling gives the same acquisition as MEI / Mars Electronics.
- **The regulatory years, dated again.** "**in 2000, I was elected… [to the] board of directors for gaming standards in Las Vegas, so I sat there for six years in a row, and then in 2006 I retired**" — consistent with the 2000–2006 GSA service already recorded.
- **Coming back to work, and the cut he made.** "**a half a year later**… **you gotta go work**… **day by day, day by day, I got rid of my CTO and CFO and got rid of fifty percent of people**", then "**we opened operations in [Colombia], Peru, in Mexico**… **the last thing I see in front of me is endless travel.**"
- **How JVL entered Georgia COAM — during COVID.** "**First time in human history, gambling and prostitution was shut down**… **I stumbled on Georgia [COAM] business. I said, 'Huh? How come it's not closed?'**" He knew the operator already — "**I knew this particular gentleman from… the late nineties, he was an investment banker**" — who told him "**we're one of the largest in… the state of Georgia… by the way, we're looking for a supplier**". Val: "**I'm starving for business**… **Five months later, had a machine in Georgia, performed very well.**"
- **On Monica**, raised by a staff member who had seen her on correspondence: "**She's a [counsel]. She's a lawyer by education**" *(the transcript renders it "consul")*.
- **On the company's move:** "**A Canadian company. In 2025, we initiated a move to the U.S. We're settled in North Miami.**"

## Sept 10 2026 (afternoon) — the same biography told again, two hours later, and one field method

Straight from the Lottery to the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-10-diamond-amusements-machine-performance-and-bundle-pilot.md|Diamond Amusement warehouse]], where he told a compressed version of the same story to a customer instead of a regulator. **The variations matter more than the overlap.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]

- **"I'm in the business for forty-two years."** Said to establish standing with technicians who had just spent half an hour listing his machines' faults. *(The corpus carries "~42 years" from a Feb-2026 meeting bio and "**45 years** of operating" from the RIS record — see [[wiki/topics/ris-remote-install-and-machine-states.md]]. All stand as spoken; none is reconciled.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **He put himself on the van, not in the office** — the point of telling it at all: "**we started as an amusement company, a bunch of countertops, and I actually was a service technician on the road, fixing jukeboxes, pinball… amusement games. So I know exactly what you're going through**… **the frustration… to be on the road [on] a hot day, and you have to commission a game or wait for [the] lottery to call back or for Felipe to call back. It's so freaking frustrating**… **It's not falling on [a] deaf ear.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **A third telling of the bill-acceptor exit, with a duration and a garbled buyer.** "**I owned a company for fifteen years that I sold to CPI, Tesco**… and I also **sat on [the] board of directors for Gaming Standards Association, developing gaming standards. I know what I'm saying.**" *("CPI, Tesco" is garbled and **no second buyer is asserted**; the Sept-2 and Sept-10-morning tellings give CPI alone. The "fifteen years" is new — the earlier tellings date the company from 1990 and the sale to 2006, which is sixteen.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **A field method he has not described anywhere else in the corpus:** "**I surveyed probably 20 locations. And none of the players have the slightest clue of what games they play**… **they feel something that they feel comfortable with, but it's not brand familiarity. They don't know what Aristocrat is.**" That is the evidence behind the claim he stated bare to [[wiki/people/narasimha-rao-namburi.md|Rao]] the day before ("the player has no concept of who the manufacturer is"). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rao_Machine_Performance_and_Exclusivity_Offer-September-9-2026.md]]
- **He absorbed an hour of complaint without arguing, and took the blame off his own man.** "**Felipe is not at fault… the problem is the design**"; "**It's my fault.**" Then explained the freeze, the Passport lock and the disabled watchdog to the customer in detail — see [[wiki/topics/egm-freezing-investigation.md]] and [[wiki/topics/ris-remote-install-and-machine-states.md]] (both Sept 10). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **And one plain statement of what the Georgia transactional push is for:** "**I'm basically buying the exclusivity of issuing cards in the location**… **my goal is to get into the card business, and that's why I'm buying your business.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]
- **He flew out that evening.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Diamond_Amusements_Machine_Performance_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pilot-September-10-2026.md]]

## Sept 11 2026 — the Friday he narrows the product, hardens two rules, and buys a regulatory answer

Five meetings that day: 8:04–9:29, 9:30–9:49, 10:33–10:52, 11:07–11:46 and 13:00–14:08 ET. He left the first one saying the second was starting.

**In the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-transactional-mvp1-scope-and-priorities.md|transactional call]]:**

- **He narrowed October 1 to one configuration, Flex IQ**, separating the two things the room had confused — moving FlexPay out of MVP 1 (a scope question) from FlexPay running in parallel (a scheduling question). He made the narrowing **conditional**: "если этого [ботлнека] нету, тогда мы пересмотрим M[V]P один." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-goes-with-flex-iq-alone-and-one-kiosk-per-location.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He asked for MudaGoose's exclusion to be confirmed on the record** — "я бы хотел бы для транскрипта, чтоб вы подтвердили" — then pressed to delete the code rather than switch it off, on the principle that "когда код остается, он всегда потом вылезает каким[-]то образом и мешает." Told it costs about 20% of the time, he moved the removal to MVP 3 instead of dropping it. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mudagoose-never-in-mvp1-and-its-code-comes-out-at-mvp3.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He extended lease blocking to the Flex-Ports** so players cannot play, and restated the standing rule in absolute terms: "автоматически мы никогда не блокируем[,] никогда" — manual only, by setting a location's lease to expired in the hub. An old misunderstanding about the Dolla Play testing was cleared up in the process. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-lease-blocking-reaches-the-flex-ports-and-is-never-automatic.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He defined what "Accepted" means on a capability** and what taking something into work means. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-accepted-means-reviewed-taken-into-work-is-the-move-to-to-do.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He said yes to autotests for the e-wallet use cases**, on the grounds that "если мы их не напишем, то это будет делаться руками[,] и руками всегда создастся ба[тлнек]", and separately advised asking Claude to produce the architecture design because it exposes holes in the capability set. See [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 11). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He ruled that only the machine goes through Eclipse** — "[т]олько машину" — closing Irina's Sept-10 question. See [[wiki/organizations/eclipse-gaming-lab.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He objected to "FlexPort" as a capability bucket name** as too narrow for e-wallet work, and suggested handing global renames to Claude since Irina lacks the rights. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He widened the weekend-overtime offer from one person to everyone**, paid as extra money rather than time off: "я бы хотел бы, чтобы все работали все дни [за] дополнительные деньги", "это значит[,] неправильно составлен график[;] нужны все, нужно всё акселер[ировать]", while conceding "я людей заставить не могу работать" and confirming "безусловно, я за это буду платить." He closed with the horizon: "с сегодняшнего дня до 15 декабря[,] у нас очень много вещей, которые нужны." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **One thing he did not settle:** Bosik's LTE-router-per-location proposal. Val argued data is cheap and a second device plus its software costs more; Bosik answered that a router needs no software. Val cut the exchange off. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]

**In the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-nova-game-change-regulatory-procedure.md|regulatory call]]:**

- **He decided to buy the answer rather than reason to it** — take the game-change question to [[wiki/organizations/gli.md|GLI]] as a paid consultation, GLI first because "**they're the most savvy and they have the most resources to explain things to us**", with a deep-knowledge participant required and himself in the room. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-the-game-change-question-goes-to-gli-as-a-paid-consultation.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_Game_Change_Regulatory_Procedure-September-11-2026.md]]
- **On the checksum:** "**Ask them to do it any other way, but I don't think it has to be one number.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_Game_Change_Regulatory_Procedure-September-11-2026.md]]
- **His week, as he described it:** "**Tuesday to Thursday, I'm seeing clients and I'm on the go all the time. And Monday, Monday night is my travel day, so I can do up until late afternoon. And Friday is my stationary day.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NOVA_Game_Change_Regulatory_Procedure-September-11-2026.md]]

### Sept 11 2026 (10:33) — he brings the customers' complaints back into engineering, and forbids fault-finding

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-technician-complaints-and-new-capabilities-workshop.md|technician-complaints workshop]] he closed the loop on ten days of operator visits by putting the complaints in writing in front of [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]] and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] and giving them four minutes to read [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Technician_Complaints_and_New_Capabilities_Workshop-September-11-2026.md]].

- **The premise, and his view of who a technician is.** "Я сейчас трачу много время, общаясь с кастами напрямую" — I am now spending a lot of time talking to customers directly — and, in English, "**Is technician to me is business owners.**" He said he had deliberately written up only the complaints that repeat constantly and are raised by everyone, and that the deepest irritations are in Georgia because Georgia is JVL's main market [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Technician_Complaints_and_New_Capabilities_Workshop-September-11-2026.md]].
- **He forbade retrospective fault-finding, explicitly.** The system is built correctly against the requirements that existed when it was built; the requirements changed radically. "я не хотел бы, чтоб мы вдруг стали в позицию, что о, что мы пропустили, как мы не то сделали" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Technician_Complaints_and_New_Capabilities_Workshop-September-11-2026.md]].
- **He admitted where his own work stopped:** prose, a deliberately broad and shallow architecture design, capabilities drawn out of it — "Но не дошел до регламента… Просто не хватило времени." **The principal is running the same artifact chain he imposes on the team, and hit the same wall.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Technician_Complaints_and_New_Capabilities_Workshop-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He corrected his own engineering director twice** on what the field actually objects to, and separated RIS from the Passport: "Не надо путать рис и паспорт." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-the-passport-is-preserved-and-jvl-is-using-the-bindings-wrongly-in-this-market.md]].
- **He ruled nothing goes into RAGE Studio** and back-ports get chosen after the capabilities exist — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-no-changes-in-rage-studio-everything-lands-on-omni.md]].
- **And he booked Monday 9:30** with Felipe, Pavel, Irina and Chudinov, undertaking to write the invitation from this meeting's own transcript — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-new-regulations-for-the-passport-and-ris-talked-out-monday-and-converted-from-the-transcript.md]].

### Sept 11 2026 (11:07) — the fourth telling of his life story, and the clearest statement of what he actually wants

Fifteen minutes later he was on a Google Meet with a payment processor he had never met ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-digital-cash-processing-card-solution-and-cash-float-partnership.md|the DCP call]]) [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

**The biography, told a fourth time in eight days — same spine, some new detail.** "**JVL is [a] family-owned company. It's forty-two years of age. I am a second generation in the business. My father started as an amusement operator**"; "**in the nineties. We owned a company called CashCode… In two thousand and six, we sold the company to CPI, which later bought MEI, which was Mars Electronics… CashCode was a technological springboard for CPI to acquire all others**"; "**in between 2000 and 2006, I sat on board of directors of Gaming Standards Association in Las Vegas. Was reelected to the board six times**"; retirement, then "**rejoined in the business half a year later, converting JVL as an amusement company. Into JVL as a slot machine manufacturer**", with offices in **Mexico, Colombia and Peru** until COVID, then the pivot to North America [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

*(Against the Sept-2 and Sept-10 tellings already on this page: the **ownership** of CashCode is given here as "we owned", not as the partnership he described to the regulator; "**forty-two years**" matches the Diamond telling and the Feb-2026 bio, against "45 years of operating" elsewhere; and the buyer chain **CPI → MEI / Mars Electronics** is stated cleanly here where Sept 10 garbled it. **All stand as spoken; none is reconciled.**)*

**New or sharper in this telling:**

- **"Two years ago, we came back into the transactional business again with a number of transactional products, which we showed in the market in Georgia for the first time this year."** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]]
- **"We have our manufacturing in Georgia, and we're licensed in South Dakota, Nebraska, Florida, and Georgia."** The clearest single statement of JVL's licensed footprint in the corpus [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].
- **"As of January 2025, we moved from Canada… To the U. S."** *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

**How he handled a stranger asking for a legal read.** He gave it as personal opinion and disclaimed authority twice — "**I'm not an authority. I'm not a legislator. I'm just like you, a technology guy**" — while stating the coming Georgia rule and its "same location" wording, and insisting on the distinction the room kept collapsing: "**The guidance is not the law, and… legislation is not the law. So the law has not passed.**" See [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Sept 11) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

**And he was candid, to strangers, that JVL's own stand caused the problem:** the device that takes cash, issues a ticket or card, lets a player walk the floor and return for cash "**as though it's an ATM machine. So we showed that device on our stand. And the… general counsel from [the Lottery] had a negative reaction to it**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

**The clearest statement in the corpus of what he wants from the payments chain**, and he cut to it himself once the product demo stalled — he asked Rick Beer what can be made on holding the money, refused to carry anyone's card without a share of the back end, gave the ATM fee to the master operator, and closed with "**My goal is to derive… my profit at… the end. From managing cash.**" Full ruling: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-market-access-traded-for-a-share-of-the-back-end-and-the-atm-fee-belongs-to-the-master-operator.md]]. His own framing of himself in the room: "**I'm only interested in money. I'm… the greedy**" *(the word "Guy." that completes the sentence falls on Rick Beer's speaker label — a Plaud mis-split; recorded, not repaired)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Digital_Cash_Processing_Card_Solution_and_Cash_Float_Partnership-September-11-2026.md]].

### Sept 11 2026 (13:00) — he ends RAGE, and then declines to overrule his designers three times

The fifth meeting of the day, the weekly [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-rage-sunset-and-class-a-on-nova.md|game-development call]], is the one where the platform decision he has been circling since August lands on a product somebody was already building. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]

- **He moved Georgia COAM Class A off RAGE onto NOVA**, discarding [[wiki/people/lena.md|Lena]]'s started work and overriding [[wiki/people/michael-gottlieb.md|Michael]]'s objection: "**I don't want to do it on Rage. It's really a bad idea to do it on Rage**"; "**Rage needs to end**"; "**Supporting 2 platforms is just… Not possible.**" His closing image: "**Rage has served so well to us for us. Let's just now park it. Kiss it, say our goodbyes, and move on.**" See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-georgia-class-a-v43-is-built-on-nova-not-rage.md]]. **This is the same ruling he made about RAGE Studio at 10:33 that morning**, applied to a product rather than to bug fixes — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-no-changes-in-rage-studio-everything-lands-on-omni.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]
- **What actually persuaded him was velocity, not architecture.** "**I'm seeing that they work with incredible speed and no[va]… It's not months, weeks. It's… [h]ours… It's mind-boggling.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He named January 1, was told it would cost Nebraska, and moved to February 1 in one exchange** — accepting Lena's sequencing without argument ("**Nebraska takes the precedence**", "**I think I can work with that**") and accepting that February 1 is a goal, not a commitment. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]
- **Three times he disagreed and three times he declined to use his position.** On duplicated progressive meters, on showing all fifteen games, and on a "new game" badge, he lost each argument and let each stand. The statement of the principle is the most explicit in the corpus: "**I'm trying to develop our own philosophy, right or wrong. And I'm hearing things from you I disagree with… And I'm not in charge of the philosophy… I disagree that we shouldn't show new game at least for three days period. But I don't direct the design, so… That's it.**" See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-no-new-game-badge-and-two-titles-per-category-stand.md]] and [[wiki/topics/slot-game-design-principles.md]] (Sept 11). **Set this against the same morning, when he twice corrected his own engineering director on what the field objects to** — his deference is to design, not across the board. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He proposed retiring a Jira item and was shown the finished design work buried under it**, then settled for a rename and a clean-up instead. His restated process rule: "**I live and die by Jira**"; and the direction of travel, "**We're going to be migrating more to GitHub… moving away from IP type of items into capabilities.**" See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-ip-887-renamed-v43-and-kept-in-backlog-not-retired.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]
- **He turned a colleague's business trip into product research:** "**Michael, I'm putting you in front of the machine and you could take all the videos you want… and you make your own decisions**" — the fix for JVL not understanding the Class A features it has to match. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]

**The tension worth flagging:** the day before he told the Georgia Lottery "**I doubt very much that I can do anything until January**" about Class A ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-no-class-a-build-until-the-january-legislature-is-heard.md]]); here he starts Class A product work immediately, for delivery after January. Neither source mentions the other; recorded under `## Open contradictions` on the decision page, not resolved. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rage_Sunset_and_Class_A_on_Nova-September-11-2026.md]]

## Sept 12 2026 — told by a customer that his product advantage will not sell, he asks for the numbers instead

A nine-minute morning phone call from [[wiki/people/yash-patel.md|Yash Patel]] of [[wiki/organizations/halcyon-amusements.md|Halcyon Amusements]] ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-12-halcyon-amusements-texas-net-win-and-lobbying.md|Sept-12, 10:04–10:13 AM ET]]), two days after that customer's first order. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]]

- **He took bad news about his own product without arguing.** Told that Texas retailers "don't understand is that the software is important" and only want "a big cabinet with a big screen," his answer was "**I hear you, and that's fine. It's better to know it up front than to be disappointed after the ship**," followed by "**A straightforward conversation is appreciated more than anything.**" *(He does not answer the substance — no product or positioning response to the warning appears in either source; the open question is on [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]].)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]]
- **His first move was to ask for the number, then to nail down its basis.** "**What is the average that you guys make in Texas?**" — then, when the operator offered "per device per location," he corrected the unit himself: "**Per device per day. Net win.**" Given "around two hundred and fifty bucks," he restated it twice more ("per machine per game", "That's **net win**, right?") before accepting it and converting it to a comparison: "**That's about two and a half times more than the average in in Georgia.**" This is the same fact-checking-at-the-table habit recorded elsewhere on this page. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]]
- **He put the Georgia baseline at "roughly a hundred dollars" a day** — a lower figure than the **$230–240/day** he was arguing for JVL's own Georgia machines three weeks earlier on Aug 21; the operator answered "**One hundred fourteen**, something like that." Neither source says whether the two figures are on the same basis (JVL's own installed machines vs a market average), and no source reconciles them. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Georgia_Machine_Sales_Strategy_Post_GOAM-August-21-2026.md]]
- **He accepted an invitation into industry politics without taking a position.** Asked to join a **unified Texas lobbying front**, he agreed to meet the following Thursday and said nothing about what JVL wants from it. The commitment and the open question are held on [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Halcyon_Amusements_Texas_Net_Win_and_Industry_Lobbying-September-12-2026.md]]

The first of Val's five recordings on Sept 14 2026 ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-texas-vertical-conversion-and-georgia-game-base.md|6:38 AM ET, Russian]]) shows him doing three characteristic things inside forty-three minutes. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]

Мы не будем стараться подгонять, мы просто возьмём игрушк[у], мы переделаем" — including the maths. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-vertical-texas-starts-from-the-georgia-coam-vertical-games.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]

Detail on [[wiki/topics/nova-platform-architecture.md]] (Sept 14). *(Neither answer is a change of plan: nothing in this source alters the RAGE sunset position or the unscheduled NOVA→GLI submission.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]
- **He ended the meeting on the grounds that more talk would waste it.** "Окей, всё остальное сегодня утром будет трата времени… Наверно всем нужно впитать всю эту информацию" — thanked Igor for the experiments ("Очень полезно"), apologised to [[wiki/people/anton-leshkevich.md|Anton Leshkevich]] for the early start, and deferred Pavel's "Юрий Валентинович" / team-sourcing topic to a separate call he undertook to make the same day. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Vertical_Conversion_and_Georgia_Game_Base-September-14-2026.md]]

## Sept 14 2026 (7:37 AM) — he writes the code standard, reads it aloud, and takes the blame for the gap it exposed

The second recording of the same morning ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-code-standards-main-branch-rule-and-alekseev-coaching.md|7:37–7:58 AM ET, Russian]]) is Val enforcing a written standard he had emailed minutes earlier.

- **He wrote the standard himself and called a meeting to have it confirmed, not obeyed.** "**Правила, которые я описал стандарты, которые описал. Они нами приняты, и они у нас, насколько я понимаю, у нас работают, да?**" — and then "**Я может быть дам вам пару минут прочитать этот email**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-main-branch-must-always-build-and-the-code-belongs-to-the-project.md]].
- **He softened his own rule the moment an engineer objected.** [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]]'s point that a developer sometimes has to merge his own pull request was accepted immediately: "**я прошу прощения… мы всегда или часто находим исключение… Но желательно, чтоб ктото другой [мержит]**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]].
- **He argued the engineering from a stated position of not being an engineer** — "**поскольку я ещё и не инженер, то мне вообще тяжело на эту тему говорить**" — and deliberately kept [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]]'s "in general" hedge rather than forcing an absolute [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]].
- **He took the specification gap as his own, in the third person.** When Pavel showed that Val's email of the previous day — Flex-Port must also work on machines without SAS — broke work already done: "**вы, кстати[,] правы, это хороший пример… тогда вал недосказал, что должен быть сортировочный экран**" — *then Val under-specified* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]]. Ruling: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-flex-port-sas-mode-is-a-selection-capability-not-a-long-lived-branch.md]].
- **He described his own way of working as the model:** small pieces, GitHub holding the versions, integration tests each time, commits "**234 раза в день**" so a reviewer never faces a week's worth of code [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]].
- **He delegated a difficult personnel conversation with instructions on tone, and closed with his father's widow-Ivanova joke** — see [[wiki/topics/jvl-operating-principles.md]] (Sept 14) and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-alekseev-is-walked-through-the-standards-by-bosik-not-ordered.md]].
- **He deferred switching off a dormant contractor account "for diplomatic reasons" while giving no work** — see [[wiki/people/eugine-yavorski.md]] (Sept 14) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Code_Standards_Main_Branch_Rule_and_Alekseev_Coaching-September-14-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** his assessments of two named engineers in this call are **deliberately not recorded** here or on their pages; only the process, engineering and resourcing facts are kept.

## Sept 14 2026 (8:01 AM) — four interventions in an hour, each of them a rule rather than an opinion

His third meeting of the morning, and by his own account a heavy day — "очень плотный день сегодня у меня[;] я ещё должен лететь." He let Klimovich run the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-transactional-mvp1-use-cases-and-ewallet-ticket-recovery.md|8:01 AM use-case call]] and intervened four times, each time converting a discussion into a standing rule.

1. **He killed manual build delivery in the first two minutes** — "Я прошу прощения, это невозможно… Я не хочу, чтобы мне чего скачивали и устанавливали" — refused Klimovich's reframing of it as an existing remote-update task, and set a 30-day target ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-nobody-downloads-and-installs-builds-thirty-day-target.md]]).
2. **He tested the TTL by attacking the cheapest case** — why hold an *empty* wallet for 30 days rather than hours — and accepted Pavel's build-cost answer on the spot ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-ewallet-ttl-defaults-to-thirty-days-and-is-set-per-location.md]]). A characteristic pattern: he does not defend a question once it has been answered.
3. **He added a requirement of his own, and accepted its deferral.** JVL must be able to measure at any moment how much paper is left, how many cards are left and how full the bill stacker is, and push that to the operator or administrator **preventively** rather than after the fact. Told there is no capability for it and that it belongs with the kiosk in MVP 2, he accepted that it was noted — detail on [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (Sept 14).
4. **He named the source of truth for the capabilities.** "[К]огда вы пишете к[е]побил[ити,] вы должны [использовать] этот SAS [standard,] а мы не просто с головой выдумываем. Это уже разработано… [Gaming Standards Association]" — and over-ruled the engineers' reach for the newest release: 6.02, because field machines lag ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-capabilities-are-written-against-the-published-sas-standard.md]]).

**He closed with process, as he had on Sept 11.** Nothing in MVP 1 may still sit in Proposed; To Do items carry features; features in progress carry a linked feature branch; capabilities in progress stay linked to requirements, and requirements carry acceptance criteria. Klimovich owns "selling" the capabilities to the developers; Bosik builds the exception report; Pavel makes sure everything In Progress is scheduled ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-nothing-in-mvp1-may-still-be-proposed-and-bosik-writes-the-exception-report.md]]). He took Pavel's correction on the In-Progress/Accepted ordering without resistance [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear for this call — no pay, rate or performance judgement of any named person appears in either source, and nothing was excluded on that ground.

## Sept 15 2026 (8:03 AM) — five rulings, one concession, and the sharpest close of the series

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-transactional-mvp1-balance-naming-and-ui-flow-walkthrough.md|Sept-15 8:03 AM call]] (Russian, 1h41m) he intervened five times and each intervention was a rule [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]].

**He refused the split, then supplied the fix.** When [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] proposed separate engineering and player vocabularies for the wallet balance, Val's "я не согласен[;] я хотел[,] чтобы это была [одна]" landed with Biratka's identical refusal, and Val's own contribution was the missing third line: "почему бы вам не добавить еще 3 линию[?] тогда станет все четко. Добавьте баланс или назовите это то[тал]". He then declared the outcome an architecture decision on the record — "**это является [ADR]. Кстати, транскрипт у нас идёт**" — and required ADRs to live in the repository. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-balance-is-total-playable-only-and-redeemable-everywhere.md]].

**He set an absolute on losing money.** "**игрок никогда не может потерять свои деньги[ —] невозможно[,] нельзя**", and then refused to let the room design what already exists: "вы сейчас тратите время на то, что уже давно разработано без нас". He named the route — [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]] first because he is free, then [[wiki/organizations/gli.md|GLI]] for a fee — and the specification, AFT plus individual states' banking law, correcting Pavel's Class-B suggestion. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-a-timeout-sends-the-money-to-unclaimed-and-the-player-never-loses-it.md]].

**He conceded once, and said so.** On a block issued by the Georgia Lottery his first position was "это не моя проблема"; Pavel refused it outright and Klimovich argued that JVL owns the e-wallet, so a player stranded with loaded money is JVL's problem. Val: "**согласен. Вы вы более гуманитарные люди, чем я**". It is the clearest instance in the corpus of him reversing inside a minute on an argument he lost [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]].

**He wrote a requirement into the room and booked it.** On cashier login: the PIN is "очень очень слабый а[кс]есс[-]контрол", so the method must be selectable between PIN and magnetic stripe card, addressed by name to [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]], and closed with "тогда давайте отразим это в кейпа билетис". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-flex-iq-cashier-flow-login-redemption-receipts-and-records.md]].

**He refused an abstract scope claim.** "**вы не можете абстрактно говорить, что чтото будет в [MVP] один без точного указания, что будет**" — and got a dated specification out of it. He applied the same impatience to Bosik's daily report: told a correction would appear tomorrow, "почему надо это завтра?… мне всё нужно сейчас или вчера" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]].

**And the close.** The demand for the non-functional requirements in writing — "**Это, если это не написано, этого нету**" — the five named owners, the three days cleared, and **Monday September 21** as the gate. He also stated the network architecture in passing: every device carries its own LTE modem and SIM. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-every-device-carries-its-own-lte-modem-and-sim.md]].

*(He twice told the recording to ignore an off-colour joke — "для транскрипта[:] это была шутка" — and it is not recorded.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]]

## Sept 15 2026 (evening) — the biography told with numbers, and the clearest statement of his regulatory doctrine

From the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-billy-liu-playpay-nda-and-georgia-float-economics.md|Sept-15 evening session]] with [[wiki/people/billy-liu.md|Billy Liu]] — nearly four hours, much of it two men swapping histories. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

### The CashCode telling, fifth version — and the first with revenue attached

> "**The company was founded in 1984, April 4, 1984. My father and I founded the company. My father and I were running the company**… **in 1994, 1990, we started a company called CashCode**… **1996, six years later, the company finally had its products. We… finished all of our R and D sampling, marketing, production, testing, and I went fully on the road, meeting customers. Nineteen ninety six. It was amazing.**"
> "**Fully consuming up until 2006 for 10 years, I was running [CashCode] entirely, sales and marketing, every single aspect of it. I basically took the company from zero sales to where it was making 60 million dollars in revenue with 22 million EBIT**… **even though we had eighteen million free cash.**"
> [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

- **A precise founding date appears for the first time: April 4 1984**, and it is given as a joint founding with his father, which sits against the "my father started the business" framing used elsewhere on this page. The CashCode start year is stated three ways in one sentence — "**in 1994, 1990**" — before he settles on the product date of 1996. The derived notes read the passage as CashCode starting **1990** with first products **1996**. **All variants stand as spoken; none is reconciled.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]
- **The revenue figures are new to the corpus** — $60M revenue, $22M EBIT, $18M free cash. Company economics, not personal compensation.
- **The buyer is named as neither Crane nor CPI.** "**this is a Craig National Bankers. Back to the one bank named Craig. And when they bought us, we became their technological springboard… into acquiring all other companies**." *(**"Craig National Bankers" / "a bank named Craig" was put to Val on 2026-09-16 and he does not know it**; the derived notes leave it unresolved and direct that it not be guessed. It sits beside the **Crane** and **CPI** buyer names already on this page as a third rendering; **any identification with "Crane" would be an inference and is not made here.**)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]
- **And the point of telling it, again, is the recycler he is selling now:** the acquirer's whole platform "**was all [garbled] on our technology, including the [bill-to-bill] recycler that I'm back to using now**" *(the transcript reads "was all deadbolt on our technology", which is not interpretable; "build-to-build" is read as **bill-to-bill**, the CashCode product name this page already carries)*; "**All designs, CPI, IT, JCM, every design is based on that design… they all use the same circuit. It's slightly different implementation.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

### When he came back to JVL

"**In twenty eighteen, this is when I came back to JVL.**" *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

*(The same passage gives **Boris**'s departure three ways inside forty seconds — "**Boris left twenty. I'm going to say twenty two or twenty three**", then "**So Boris left twenty eighteen, and I came back. And Peter left years later, twenty nineteen**" — and calls 2019 "exactly during COVID", which it is not. The derived notes settle on **Boris 2018, Peter 2019**. **The transcript contradicts itself; the notes' reading is recorded as the notes' reading, not asserted as fact.**)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

**On the company's origins:** "**The company is Canadian. We originated from Toronto, but never had any business in Canada.**" See [[wiki/organizations/jvl.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]].

### Why he prefers the street market to casinos, in one line

> "**Casinos have attitude. Go ahead. Put machines as many as you want. I'm not paying. And I'm not profit sharing. So why am I?**"

Said in answer to Billy's "**when you deal with these properties and casinos… they got you by the balls. They don't pay you**" and "**they have this Walmart supplier mentality**." Val's own summary of his history with them: "**I spoke to you about them. I pulled out.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Mutual_NDA_Renegotiation-September-15-2026.md]]

### The regulatory doctrine, stated to an outsider who was doing the opposite

Told a competitor who calls the Lottery's opinion letter "a stupid letter", Val argued the reverse posture — go to the regulator first, get clearance, and treat the card network as the real chokepoint: "**all the [lottery] has to do is to call Visa and… say… this individual that's clearing transactions with you is breaking criminal law**… **it's better to play with the legislator straight.**" With it came the anti-money-laundering argument that made Billy concede, and the advice to "**mute**" rather than "remove" the contested feature. Full account on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].

### Two admissions he does not make to customers

- **The transactional bundle has sold nothing yet.** "**I just started after the show. I don't have even one installation. Not even one. And maybe I will fail.**" That is thirteen days into the customer run recorded across [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].
- **What he demonstrated at the August show was faked.** "**I faked that… What I show them is a fake circle where I get a win from a temporary wallet… I fake issue the black card**… **Did you use real cards? I didn't, but I could.**" And his belief about the consequence: "**This letter is about me.**" See [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] and [[wiki/people/joe-kim.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Georgia_Payment_Ecosystem_and_Float_Economics-September-15-2026.md]].

## Sept 16 2026 (6:11 AM) — he offers to fund a Texas venture, describes himself as a wolf, and then refuses to close it

A 37-minute breakfast in Lawrenceville, Georgia with an outside route operator, [[wiki/people/dannettee-hickman.md|Dannettee Hickman]] — [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-dannettee-hickman-texas-partnership-exploration.md]]. It is the only source in the corpus where Val proposes that **JVL own and run its own gaming locations** rather than sell into someone else's. The terms are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-texas-front-operator-model-not-an-equal-partnership.md]]; the market thread is on [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]

- **He led with the market's weakness, not the opportunity.** "Just letting you know that **Texas is volatile**… **Some people are saying the collections are close to $250 per day per machine net win. Others are saying collections are twenty-five dollars per day per machine. I'm confused. It doesn't jive.**" Four days after being handed the $250 figure by a Texas operator, he is using it as a warning to a counterparty rather than as a planning number. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]
- **The terms, stated before any agreement was sought.** "**We will not be equal partners… I'm looking for somebody to be a front man to open up locations. I will not be splitting money with you fifty-fifty because it will be mainly my investment.**" And the division of labour: "**Back office is not my issue. Management is not my issue. I'm very, very good at it**… **I will know how much each machine makes before you wake up.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]
- **The self-description, given as full disclosure.** "**I am a wolf. I take no prisoners. I will step all over you. I will absolutely kill you if you fuck around. No mercy. And I will do it with a competitor. I will do it with a friend. I will do it with anybody.**" With the rules he says follow from it: "**I never bullshit. I never lie. Because I have a bad memory. I don't remember what I said**", and "**I can get to the point within the first 15 minutes. My meetings are very short. Most people don't like it.**" The last line is a rare explicit statement of the meeting style visible across this entire corpus. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]
- **And then he refused the deal he had just described.** "**Don't give me any answers. We don't need to shake hands on anything. Let's leave it open**… **I prefer no decision than a decision.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]

### The Colombian route — the model he says he is copying

An extended anecdote, told to explain what he wants built in Texas. A young man, broke, in **Colombia in the 1980s** — "I'm going to build a slot machine road" — starts buying used "**valley**" slot machines *(the transcript's rendering; **not** resolved to any manufacturer here)*, supplied via a New-York-connected intermediary Val was introduced to him through, **in Miami**. Colombia was unregulated and "the cartel was raging at that time… **Luckily, they were not much interested in the gambling proceeds.**" The finale, which is the part Val is after: "He keeps on developing his own locations. **Starts from renting spots. Eventually, he starts buying up those spots when the leases come to an end**… **secures long term leases**… **In 2010, he sells his entire business. Fifty percent sells fifty percent of his business to BlackRock**… **for crazy amounts of money, retains the real estate that the locations were in, leases those locations to BlackRock.**" *(The "entire business / fifty percent" wobble is Val's own self-correction in the same breath; the derived notes read it as **50%**, and the sentence settles there. Quoted whole rather than cleaned.)* **The man is never named.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]

### The biography, sixth telling in a fortnight — and the first with the Canadian operating years in detail

This telling adds material the page did not hold. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dannettee_Hickman_Texas_Gaming_Partnership_Exploration-September-16-2026.md]]

- **Why the Canadian business was legal, and how big it was.** "**Canada, up until 1996, had no criminal statute of gambling, of slot machine gambling.** I didn't know that. **I was the largest operator in Canada, operating video horse racing machines**" — "**and a liners**" [eight-liners]. He frames the whole start as "I started my business from. Being totally great" — garbled, and the sense is not recoverable. *(The "largest operator in Canada" claim is new to the corpus; recorded as his own words. The **1996** date is not new: in the earlier Jan-8-2026 lunch telling he already said the grey business "died in 1996" when "it became a criminal offense to have a machine" — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rich_Winley_Miami_Lunch_Texas_Distribution_Pitch-January-8-2026.md]].)*
- **The bill-acceptor problem that created CashCode.** "**I was the first one to install bill acceptors in Canada. There was no bill acceptor. We have colored money, right? Our money is colored. And there was no bill acceptor that would read the money. So there was only one bill acceptor that we had to adapt.**"
- **The 1990 origin, matching the Sept-10 telling and not the Sept-2 one.** "In **nineteen ninety**, I had one of my customers come to me and say, '**I know that you're using a lot of bill acceptors. I can manufacture an amazing bill acceptor**'… **I said no**, this is I'm not getting into this shit. I don't need it. **So he went and convinced my father** that we needed to invest into this business, and so **we took fifty percent partnership**… **Three years later, he brought a prototype in, and three years after that, we became profitable.**" He was, at the time, "**swimming in money**… like a bull in a china shop." *(Three-plus-three from 1990 lands on **1996** for profitability — which independently matches the Sept-15 telling's "**1996, six years later, the company finally had its products**." The two are consistent; noted, not merged.)*
- **The scale of the company, in numbers this page did not have.** "**It took money from 17 countries. We had 35 partners**… **We employed 350 people.** It's just crazy business. **He ran the back shop, and I ran the front.**" And the travel: "this is when I started to **live sixty percent out outside of home. We did twenty-five trade shows a year**… I had **two trade show crews working around the clock**, tracking my shipments, setting up and dismantling trade shows, and **I would show up only for two days, fly in.**" *(The 25-trade-shows figure matches his Jun-16 2026 telling already on this page; the 17 countries / 35 partners / 350 employees are new.)*
- **The exit, as before.** "**in 2006, I sold the company for massive amounts of money.**" *(No buyer is named in this source, so it adds nothing to the standing CPI / Crane / "a bank named Craig" question above.)*

## Sept 16 2026 (8:01 AM) — he reverses the engineering position on offline operation, and makes honesty about October 1 a rule

The third MVP 1 morning call ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-transactional-mvp1-open-questions-and-offline-operation.md|Sept 16 2026 8:01 AM call]], Russian). Two instruments, both characteristic.

**First, he refused to let a location stop.** Told that Flex IQ was specified never to run without a connection, he put the product-owner obligation above the control requirement: "**с точки зрения [про]дакт[-]о[у]нера, вы не можете позволить, чтобы лакейш[н] не работал**", with the commercial consequence stated plainly — "**нас выгонят с лакейш[н]ов**" (we will be thrown out of the locations) — and the business restated at its end point: "**конечный итог должен быть тот, что мы печатаем деньги. Ни тикеты[,] а деньги**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]]. The settlement is [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-a-location-keeps-working-without-internet-and-only-the-card-stops.md]].

**He set the offline window himself, and doubted it in the same breath.** Against Klimovich's "unlimited" and Pavel's "until the lease expires", Val listed "или 6, или 12, или 24" and chose 24 hours as a working figure — "пока не принято это решение. Давайте 24" — while saying "**я согласен, что 24 может быть ту[ ]лон[г][;] если у кого[-]то есть какие[-]то критические взгляды на это, пожалуйста[,] я опен**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

**Second, he made October 1 a filter on the whole requirement set:** "**уберите это. Надо быть честн[ым] с самим собой. Мне важно, чтобы работал[о] 1 октября. Всё, что мы не можем сделать 1 октября, уберите**" and "**не надо… самим себе врать нельзя**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-anything-that-depends-on-the-cloud-leaves-mvp1.md]].

**The one idea of his the engineers refused**, and his standing complaint about that. He floated printing a validation ticket offline, or pre-generating about a hundred validation IDs in advance; Chudinov called it "очень плохой кейс" and [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] agreed. Val's reply is a pattern worth recording: "**я уже много раз сталкивался… с таким разговором[:] ни один инженер никогда ещё не доказывал, какие проблемы**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]]. He did not press it.

**He also closed the cloud e-wallet down on schedule grounds** — "мы говорим про релиз в 2027[,] в 28 год[у]… мы… 2 [часа] потратим на это и никуда не придём" — and closed the meeting by restating the focus rule unchanged: "[и]нженер[ы] продолжа[ют] работать на одном [функциональном рек]в[а]йрмент[е]… кроме [этого] никто ничего не программирует" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]]. On capacity he separated the release number from the design number: 15 Flex-Ports for MVP 1, "систем[е] нужен дизайн на 99" ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-fifteen-flex-ports-in-mvp1-and-the-system-designed-for-ninety-nine.md]]) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

## Sept 16 2026 (12:35 PM) — "I am speaking for the transcript", a travel plan, and a staffing intent the notes dropped

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-street-machine-board-spec-and-georgia-class-a-order.md|Sept-16 12:35 PM alignment call]] Val spent an hour dictating street-machine requirements to two engineers, took two phone calls out of the middle of it, and stated plainly why he was talking the way he was.

**The register he was working in, said twice.** Challenged by [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]] for specifying method rather than requirement, he answered: "**я говорю для … записи, чтобы можно было выпустить и [проголосовать], поэтому я говорю, что всё в голову приходит**" and, later, "**я не говорю как делать. Я просто сейчас говорю для транскрипта все вещи, которые могут понадобиться… Безусловно, я не диктую как делать**" — he is putting requirements into the record so they can be written up and voted on, not issuing instructions [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Board_Spec_and_Georgia_Class_A_Order-September-16-2026.md]].

**He separated his own opinion from field evidence, explicitly.** On where the electronics sit: "**я с вами разговаривал ещё на эту тему давно. Но это было моё мнение, а сейчас я слышу это мнение уже [от техников]**" — and he offered to send Genadi the recording of the technicians or take him to meet them. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-street-machine-electronics-move-to-eye-level-and-the-monitor-opens-like-a-cupboard-door.md]].

**He also reversed himself in public, twice on the same call** — on low-power mode (absolutely not needed → keep it, it is already written and tested) and on the certification enclosure (no box → "**сейчас я как[-]то [гребу] обратно**"). Both reversals, with his reasoning, are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-one-fram-chip-instead-of-six-and-low-power-mode-laid-in-but-not-populated.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-street-machines-skip-gli-so-no-boxes-and-no-tampers-but-nebraska-reopens-it.md]].

**Travel plan, as given to the team:** Texas on the **2nd**, Nebraska on the **5th, 6th and 7th**, back in Georgia on the **12th**. **The month is not stated in the source** and is not inferred here [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Board_Spec_and_Georgia_Class_A_Order-September-16-2026.md]].

**Two absences from his own meeting.** He left at 00:15:01 ("**я уже взялся телефон**") and returned 16½ minutes later apologising — "**Я ожидал этот звонок уже много времени. Наконец[-]то мне этот человек позвонил, и я не мог отказать**" — then left again at 00:45:21 ("**мне нужно общаться на звонок, продолжайте**") for a further nine minutes. The first absence maps exactly to a separate Sept-16 recording made in that window; see the §12 note on the meeting stub [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Board_Spec_and_Georgia_Class_A_Order-September-16-2026.md]].

### A staffing intent that is absent from the derived notes

Recovered from the transcript only — **the derived notes do not contain this passage at all**. Immediately after describing the button panel, Val laid out how he wants the work resourced [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Board_Spec_and_Georgia_Class_A_Order-September-16-2026.md]]:

> "**Я хочу обойти … наш [graphic] department[,] я не хочу, чтобы они ничего делали. Я хочу в Минске взять одного человека. Я хочу привести Сергея Клемёныча на работу обратно … [ч]тоб он работал в паре с каким[-]то программистом или даже[,] может быть, сам всё делал… [е]го можно научить [Claude Code]. Давайте какую[-]то помощь там местную… Вот такой мой план.**"

- **Recorded:** the work is to be routed around JVL's graphics department; **one person hired in Minsk**; **"Сергей Клемёныч" brought back to work**, paired with a programmer or working alone; that person taught the AI coding tool (transcript "**склодам**"; read as **Claude Code** — an inference, and the same tool reference appears elsewhere in this thread).
- **§4 — resolved the next day (updated Sept 17 2026).** When this passage was written, "**Сергей Клемёныч**" matched no person in the corpus and was queued for the owner. The **Sept-17 12:45 PM call supplies the match**: Val there instructs the team to hire the same man — rendered "**климеча**" by Val and "**кремёнычев**" by [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], and "Kleimenich" in that meeting's derived notes — from Monday, and both Pavel and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] call him "Сергей". He is read as [[wiki/people/sergey-kleimenychev.md|Sergey Kleimenychev]], the Minsk graphic-design generalist who appears on the Minsk payroll roster as "Kleimenychev Sergey". The four grounds for the reading, and its residual uncertainty, are set out on that page; **the identification is still queued for one word of owner confirmation**, because no speaker anywhere spells the surname or links the two mentions. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Kleimenich_Hire_Nova_Direction_and_Planning_Handover-September-17-2026.md]]
- **Ambiguous scope.** Whether "our graphics department" means the game-art team or cabinet design is not determinable from the source. **Not resolved here.** *(The Sept-17 call does not settle it either: the assignment Val gives is "Texas and Class A", with no department named.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Kleimenich_Hire_Nova_Direction_and_Planning_Handover-September-17-2026.md]]
- **§15.8 — one element held.** Val's evaluative remark about why he wants this particular person back is a performance judgement about a named individual and is **not recorded**. The staffing intent itself is business direction, and is.

On [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-jvl-transactional-ecosystem-and-class-a-market-strategy.md|the afternoon strategy session]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_JVL_Transactional_Ecosystem_and_Class_A_Market_Strategy-September-16-2026.md]]:

- **Why he keeps the machine business separate:** "**to make machines is very difficult and I don't want to bring it into the pot of soup with something else. I just like the exclusivity. I like to be in the machine business**… **Transactional business is not creative; it's mechanical. It was easy for me to enter because of my cash card experience.**" He also cites "**sitting on at GSA board**" as part of that background *(as transcribed; not expanded here)*.

- **The install crunch is November, not October.** "**not even October, but the month of November, you'll be swamped. You'll be literally swamped with installations. My only hope and my pray is that we, in November, we have a good chance to have Wi-Fi configuration. So you may roll out twenty, twenty of them at the time**"; and on people, "**I'm very happy that [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andre]] will be back and [[wiki/people/sebastian.md|Sebastian]] will be here.**"
- **And he is moving a technician in from Nebraska:** "**I'm bringing another guy. I got him from Nebraska, but Nebraska is not busy right now… He's a worker type of a guy… he's going to live with this equipment and see locations here… He's a very quiet guy, but very smart guy. His name was also Andre.**" *(**A second Andre** — not [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], who is named separately in the same breath. No surname is given; **not matched to anyone in this corpus and no page created** (§4). The [[wiki/projects/nebraska-skill-market.md|Nebraska]] slowdown is his stated reason.)*

## Sept 17 2026 (8:49 AM, Roswell) — ninety minutes of relationship-building, and the clearest statement of why he will not take a side

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-halcyon-amusements-georgia-class-a-and-texas-route-partnership.md|Halcyon breakfast]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Halcyon_Amusements_Georgia_Class_A_and_Texas_Route_Partnership-September-17-2026.md]]:

- **Personal view against company view, said out loud to a customer.** "**Let me tell you personally, and this is Val speaking… I want gift card. I want ATM. I want cash. But that's me personally… The view of my company is this: I want longevity of the business. Because I want to sell today, tomorrow, and I want to build relationships.**" And the fear that follows from it: "**The split of the market is more dangerous than everybody going in one direction. I almost prefer everybody going in whatever direction they pick.**"
- **His prescription for the SkillPay conflict is silence.** "**This conflict should have been left in the back room, not in public… Issue your… cards. Don't make any statements**"; "**the more anybody asks for clarity or justification… Stop asking. Just stop digging.**" Full passage on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] (Sept 17 2026).
- **His model of how an attorney general will behave** — the one piece of political reasoning he offers: an AG with ambition "of becoming a governor… and he or she deals with a **Bible Belt state**" has every reason to prosecute; an AG who "**wants tax dollars**" has every reason to let it run and tax it. His timing: "**Not before election. Nothing's going to happen.**"
- **The Canadian grey-market history he uses as his credential for the whole argument.** "**From 1986 to 1996, JVL was the largest grey operator here in Canada… because up until 1996 there was no federal criminal law that defined a slot machine.**" His own path: "**I started as a game mechanic. I was an operator. I was a distributor. Manufacturer. And then… I sat on the legislative board for video lottery, and then I sat on [the] Gaming Standards Association board and reelected six times. I know what you guys are going through.**" His advice from it: "**take a chill pill… Keep on quietly making your money. Move on.**"
- *(Personal matters removed — owner ruling 2026-09-28.)*
- **A Lawrenceville building, bought or leased — the source says both.** "**I just bought a small building here in Lawrenceville. Maybe it's gonna give me eight percent cap. It's nothing, but I need it for JVL, so I just bought it**"; then, of the same building and seller, "**He'll rent it to you, and he's willing to sell. So I signed the leasing contract with him for two years.**" Size: "**ten thousand square feet.**" The seller is "**an Indian guy from the industry… His name is Raheel**", introduced by [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]. **Bought and leased are both stated in the same passage and the source does not reconcile them; neither is preferred and no ownership is asserted.** *(Back-fill, Mar 9 2026: Val told Rich Winley "We rent this building originally, and now we're buying it at the end of the year" of a Lawrenceville building. Whether it is the same building is not said; this resolves nothing.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rich_Winley_Texas_Rollout_Delay_and_Georgia_Buyers-March-9-2026.md]]
- **He tells the JVL-enters-Georgia origin story.** "**I am still friends with Lucky Bucks… they said we need equipment… I said, where the… is Georgia? He says, come down, we'll show you. So I came down. [I thought] this is paradise… Make equipment. Took me three months. I gave them equipment. They tested, wonderful… Start buying container [after] container.**" Asked what happened to them: "**Creative financing.**" *(He names the parent as **Seven Aces Holdings** in agreement with his counterpart. **Note §4: Lucky Bucks / Seven Aces is the company Val separately ruled is now [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md|ARC]] — it is NOT [[wiki/organizations/lucky-coin.md|Lucky Coin]], a different and smaller Georgia master. Do not conflate.)*
- **He asked for the business directly and was told no, then negotiated a smaller yes.** "**So I don't think anybody could beat my price. So, why can't I get your business?**" — and, on being told the locations are already fitted, "**Why don't you sell [the incumbent's] equipment?… Why don't you try couples?**" See the [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-halcyon-takes-one-machine-plus-the-free-transactional-package-first-week-of-november.md|decision page]].
