## Sept 17 2026 (8:02 AM, in absentia) — invited to the MVP 1 call, present in it only as two standing rules

He was invited to the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-transactional-mvp1-skillpay-multi-product-and-device-network-security.md|fourth consecutive MVP 1 morning call]] and **did not speak on it at all** — no turn anywhere in the ninety-minute recording. It is the first of the four mornings without him, and the meeting ran to five voices instead of seven. His own two recordings that day, the 7:59 J&J call and the 8:49 Roswell breakfast, are separate conversations that merely overlap it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]

**He was nevertheless cited twice, by two different people, as the authority for a rule.**

- **[[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] on the Monday gate:** "**я помню, что вал говорил о том, что… условно к понедельнику нам надо ещё продумать всю вот эту вещь, связанную с оперированием устройств по удалёнке**" — by Monday the team must also have thought through operating the devices remotely. Klimovich used that to decide that signal quality means *remote* visibility for MVP 1 ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-signal-quality-in-mvp1-means-remote-visibility-not-a-screen.md]]), and it adds a fourth item to the Sept-21 grooming gate he set ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]]). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]
- **[[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] on the watchdog:** "**то, что вал тоже говорил, что во[тч]дог должен быть во всех устройствах. Если он вешается, он должен выходить в ребут и загружаться с нуля**" — a universal rule for every device, implemented on the gaming machines but written against the FP-10 and therefore not transferable to Flex IQ, which is why it went to MVP 2 ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-bosiks-network-list-splits-between-mvp1-and-mvp2.md]]). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]

*(Both are reports of Val's instructions by other people, not his own words on this recording, and are recorded as such.)*

## Sept 17 2026 (12:19 PM) — "I am not interested in the artist's intent, I am interested in hard geometry and physics"

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-vertical-game-skeletons-and-the-art-pipeline.md|12:19 PM game-conversion session]] (Russian) he watched a vertical game play and then spent the rest of the call re-specifying how art should reach code. The ruling is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-hard-geometry-from-the-scene-files-and-a-vertical-skeleton-per-game-format.md]]. Three things about **how he works** show clearly here [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Game_Skeletons_and_Art_Pipeline-September-17-2026.md]]:

- **He builds his model out loud and lets it be corrected.** He described how he imagined artists hand work to programmers, Igor told him that is not how it is done, and Val kept the part of his idea that survived — that the intent has already become numbers, and the numbers are what he wants: "**меня интересует жёсткая геометрия и физика … где она написана, где это описано.**" He twice checked he was being understood ("**я по-русски правильно объяснил? Я могу другими словами**") and invited Igor to play it back ("**давайте я скажу, как я вас понял**").
- **He is contemptuous of unquantified design talk, and says so coarsely.** A designer saying "a little green here, a little red there" is, in his words, what he cannot listen to; the graphic designers' gesturing he dismissed outright as people "**которые не знают, как объяснить геометрию, механику, текстуру**". The object of the contempt is the *method*, not a named person — no individual is criticised in the source.
- **He will not push a person past a stated limit, but says plainly that he could.** Offered Saturday and told Igor to choose the day himself: "**вы мне говорите, чтобы я вас не … потому что я могу выжить из людей [все] соки**" — I can squeeze people dry (the elided verb is coarse) — then "**подумайте, напишите мне, когда с вами встретится**". He flies on Friday and may be reachable from the airport if he arrives early with somewhere to sit.

He closed by praising the result and crediting it to everyone except its author — "**классный результат … и обошлись без Антона**" — and accepted the correction from Irina and Pavel that the video was Anton's ("**опять Антон**"). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Game_Skeletons_and_Art_Pipeline-September-17-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — no compensation, rate or HR judgement is recorded from this session; Val's remarks about design method are recorded as direction, not as a performance assessment of any named person.

## Sept 17 2026 (12:45 PM) — three rulings in seven minutes, and a silence taken as consent

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-kleimenychev-hire-nova-direction-and-planning-handover.md|12:45–12:52 PM ET "General alignment" call]], two minutes after the `Game_Conversion_progress` session ended, Val settled three things in sequence with [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]]. It is a compact example of how he closes a question when he does not want it discussed. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Kleimenich_Hire_Nova_Direction_and_Planning_Handover-September-17-2026.md]]

- **The hire, with himself as the reporting line.** Hire the man from Monday, push "**Техас и класс эй**", keep it "**В полном в полном секрете**" — and "**А командовать парадом буду я**", I will run the show. He also refused the candidate's one request outright ("**Что абсолютно нет**"): no consulting [[wiki/people/lena.md|Lena]] at any point. Full record, and the §15.8 exclusions, on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-kleimenychev-is-hired-from-monday-onto-texas-and-class-a-in-full-secrecy.md]].
- **The platform, closed by silence.** Told that the running experiment produces RAGE output that would later need converting again into NOVA, he ruled "**не делать никаких игр в речь**" and "**сразу переключиться на нового и Техас сделать на ново**". Nobody answered for about nine seconds; he closed it himself — "**Я понял, что вы согласны, поэтому идём в этом направлении**" — and added "**У меня нету времени нету, время на полемику нету время на полемику**". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-no-more-games-on-rage-and-texas-is-built-on-nova.md]].
- **A job taken off a direct report.** "**Паша с вас нужно снять.**" … "**Планирование.**" — planning goes to an application administrator, with Pavel and Irina feeding him the plans in recorded sessions. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-planning-comes-off-pavel-and-goes-to-an-application-administrator.md]].

**His stated risk posture, in his own idiom:** "**100% только таксы и смерть.**" … "**Всё остальное.**" … "**Подлежит.**" … "**Проценту вероятности.**" — only taxes and death are certain; everything else is a matter of probability. He was answering Pavel's condition that the experiment can be run provided nobody expects a guaranteed result. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Kleimenich_Hire_Nova_Direction_and_Planning_Handover-September-17-2026.md]]

**§15.8 — a large part of what he said on this call is not recorded.** The bulk of his speech consists of **personal assessments of named individuals** — the man being hired, Lena, and Pavel. None of it is ingested here or anywhere. No compensation material of any kind appears in either source.

## Sept 17 2026 (1:47 PM) — he inspects a finished unit, and states the arithmetic he applies to every hardware quote

The last recorded session of his Sept-17 ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-flexpay-slim-lid-critique-and-flexport-landed-cost.md|1:47–1:57 PM, with Genadi Peari]], Russian, two segments). Two things about how he works appear here in an unusually plain form.

**He inspects finished product himself, and the standard is appearance.** With a built FlexPay Slim in front of him he read out a list — unglued ports, boards lying exposed, a heap of wires, a taut string holding the lid — and gave the whole list the same frame: "**я просто говорю, что я вижу**", and "**Я убедительно прошу вас сделать это элегантно и красиво и индустриально, потому что… Мы начинаем установки всех этих вещей, это будет критики полно**". His fix is deliberately cheap and visible — a flat metal mesh on standoffs, "**стоила нам буквально копейки, но эстетику придала просто колоссальны**" — and he pays for the mechanism without argument: the damped lid "**на какието доллары увеличить стоимость, но элегантность… совершенно на другом уровне**". He also **declined to specify the behaviour** once the principle was set, handing the self-open-versus-no-slam choice back to Genadi: "**Я оставляю это для вас, как вы хотите**". **Inference, labelled:** this is the same "specify the building blocks and hand the rest to the professionals" posture recorded from his Nov-19 2025 call with Genadi on [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md]]; no source connects the two. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-a-metal-mesh-on-standoffs-covers-the-flexpay-slim-service-bay-and-legs-replace-the-string.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Pay_Slim_Lid_and_Wiring_Critique-September-17-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Pay_Slim_Lid_and_Wiring_Critique-September-17-2026.md]]

**He carries a private markup on every hardware number he is given, and said so twice.** "**Я добавляю 30%**", repeated verbatim a minute later, with the explanation: "**Когда я вас спрашиваю[ про] м[атериал], это это без всяких[,] без Фре[йта][,] без… всего остального, а на самом деле я должен добавлять больше[ — ]я должен добавлять около 35%**". Nothing was changed; the 35% is stated as the truer figure, not adopted. His answer to the underlying problem was structural rather than arithmetic — create an import-duties tax category in Business Central so the landed cost is held in the system. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-import-duty-gets-its-own-tax-category-in-business-central.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Board_Landed_Cost_and_Import_Duty-September-17-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_FlexPort_Board_Landed_Cost_and_Import_Duty-September-17-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — the call is a criticism of a unit, and only the engineering faults are recorded; no judgement of the person who built it, and no compensation material in either segment.

## Sept 17 2026 (3:17 PM) — a long sell to a Georgia master, a design hole admitted in the room, and no discount on an old debt

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-dynamic-gaming-georgia-master-licensee-and-cashless-pitch.md|Dynamic Gaming meeting]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dynamic_Gaming_Georgia_Master_Licensee_and_Cashless_Pitch-September-17-2026.md]]:

- **He stayed a day for it.** "**I only stayed for you. I just wanted to meet you, so I changed my flight. I'm going to fly tomorrow morning.**"
- **His credential, in this version:** "**I'm in the business for 42 years… I started as a machine mechanic in location, repairing jukeboxes and mechanical bingo machines… then I was an operator… I also worked in three countries in Latin America. I worked all over Canada**". *(Compare the Roswell version the same morning, above.)*
- **He admitted a design gap to the customer's face.** On unclaimed funds: "**Good point. It's a it's a hole in our… design**", and "**we absolutely have no issue keeping you for seven years**". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-ewallet-ttl-defaults-to-thirty-days-and-is-set-per-location.md]] (Sept 17).
- **He priced the bundle as "buying the market"** and would not give this master a better price — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-the-ninety-dollar-fee-is-drafted-from-the-location-not-paid-by-the-master.md]].
- **He refused to discount a year-old receivable.** "**If I will show my books to my investors and… they say, what is it bad debt that you're discounting? I don't want to declare that it's bad debt… I can't discount a year-old account**" — offering breaks on new business instead.
- **Data he does not want to hold.** "**I don't want JVL to be in position where we keep somebody's records, and God forbid… my employee steals it**"; he offered an NDA and to destroy the data on request.
- **He reads himself, in the debrief:** "**I have a tendency of falling in love with my own conviction… so I try to keep balance between falling in love and being realistic**", and he accepted Felipe's point that the giveaway was played too late. Detail on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]] (Sept 17).

## Sept 18 2026 (8:03 AM, in the chat) — he attends the last MVP 1 call in writing, and gets one instruction obeyed and one contested

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-18-transactional-mvp1-capability-cleanup-and-the-monday-deadline.md|fifth and final MVP 1 morning call]] before his own Monday Sept 21 grooming gate (Russian). **He carries no speaker turn anywhere in the recording.** He took part by writing in the meeting chat, and his messages were read out loud in the room [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**Three interventions reach the recording:**

1. **He asked for the capability list to be walked again.** When [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]] objected that they had already done it, [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]'s first reason was "**вопервых[,] Вал предлагает это делать**". The whole cleanup sweep that follows is his instruction being carried out.
2. **Obeyed — drop the rollback requirement.** [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] read it out mid-argument: "**Вал написал[:] убрать из [MVP] один[,] убрать[,] и продолжим дальше**". Klimovich: "**Всё понятно, давайте уберём**". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-no-rollback-to-a-previous-build-and-the-team-rolls-forward-only.md]].
3. **Capability 178 must be in MVP 1 — his instruction, later withdrawn by him.** Irina read "**178 — [маст би MVP] один**", Pavel confirmed "**Это текст от Вала**", and Klimovich answered with a near-final position going the other way: two InComm products will not be built for MVP 1, with a single-product (SkillPay) fallback offered instead. **Nobody reconciled the two, and it is the one unresolved scope item going into his Monday gate** — recorded as an open contradiction on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]]. **→ SETTLED by owner ruling 2026-09-20: "Klimovich is correct" — capability 178 / two-product support is NOT in MVP 1; Val's own chat instruction is withdrawn. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]].**

**His Monday deadline was restated by the team without him** — "**У нас был дедлайн, пред[ъявленный] перед нами**" — and Klimovich added the rule that follows it, binding himself: from Monday nothing more enters MVP 1. Val's own commitment to run that grooming meeting is untouched and still open ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]]). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]]

*(A pattern now two days old: on Sept 17 he was invited and did not attend at all, and his influence reached the room only through other people quoting him. On Sept 18 he is present but silent, steering in writing. In both cases the record of what he wanted is second-hand — spoken by whoever read the chat.)* **Labelled as an observation, not a source claim.**

## Sept 20 2026 (7:04 AM, Sunday) — "the first time in twenty years we approached game visuals correctly"

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-20-vertical-geometry-standard-and-adr-consolidation.md|Sunday games-conversion session]] (Russian) he overruled [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] and ruled that Igor's vertical geometry standard, a glossary and ADRs go into GitHub as an experiment "**в вакууме**", outside any framework — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-20-the-vertical-geometry-standard-glossary-and-adrs-go-to-github-outside-any-framework.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]

- **His reason:** "**это в 1 раз … где мы правильно … определили подход к разработке игры … За все наши годы … Там 20 лет**". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]
- **ADRs first.** "**[ADR]s, это самое важное, что существует здесь**"; extract them from all the transcripts with Claude and keep them in the repository with the code. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]
- **Numbers, not adjectives.** He wants colour described as a move from one Pantone range to another over a set time or frame count — "**чтобы мы перестали говорить немножко синенький, немножко оранжевый**" — and a start on the physics of materials and motion. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]
- **Designers out.** "**я не хочу делиться с дизайнерами и никакими нашими подходами**"; new art, if needed, from [[wiki/people/sergey-kleimenychev.md|Sergey Kleimenychev]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]
- **A stopwatch test.** He wants to time Claude converting a horizontal RAGE game into a working vertical NOVA game from the standards alone — but only after GitHub is organised and end-to-end testing is finished, in that order. He set the GitHub work for **Thursday** (Sept 24 by date) and a one-hour meeting at 8:00 AM Eastern. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Vertical_Geometry_Standard_and_ADR_Consolidation-September-20-2026.md]]

## Sept 22 2026 (6:02 PM, Plaud) — he asks an old designer to pull JVL's look together, and tells him the market story

On a [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-22-howard-tikka-brand-realignment-and-collateral.md|22-minute call]] with [[wiki/people/howard-tikka.md|Howard Tikka]] he ruled that JVL's look is to be collected into one package with the logo and slogan untouched — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-22-unify-jvl-look-logo-and-slogan-untouched.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]

- **Why he is doing it himself.** "JVL is still small and crawling… I'm actively plowing and plowing, and I'm back on the road and meeting clients and basically back to square one." And: "I'm running engineering and sales and marketing." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]
- **The casino market, as he told it.** "over COVID, casinos realized that they don't need to have five thousand machines on the floor. They can make just as much money with four thousand or three thousand… The casino business is not a growing business. It's a mature business. So there's no market in class three gaming." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]
- **The street market instead.** "Georgia is a street market. Nebraska is a street market. Even if you remember South Dakota VLT, it's a street market… Georgia has forty-seven thousand machines and seven thousand four hundred locations." Howard: "It's essentially going back to where JVL started." Val: "Exactly." The same Georgia counts are on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]
- **Transactional products.** "JVL is expanding into transactional products. In a way, it's somewhat of a CashCode trend, a little bit, not fully." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]
- **A container a month.** "I had a relationship from twenty-five years ago, and this guy turned out to be a CEO of the second-largest company in Georgia… It was buying a container of machines per month from me for a year." The man and the company are not named. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]
- **Moving south.** "We're in process of departing Canada… We're moving to the states." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Howard_Tikka_Brand_Realignment_and_Collateral-September-22-2026.md]]

## Sept 22 2026 (8:01 AM) — back in the MVP 1 call: he challenges the date, names FlexPay, and asks for one test by tomorrow

He chaired the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-22-transactional-mvp1-jira-cleanup-and-e2e-tests.md|Sept-22 8:01 AM MVP 1 call]] (Russian), having missed two calls by his own account [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]]:

- **He defers to the product owner on screens** — "Легко воспринимаю Андрея требования как догма сейчас" — and gave two wording rulings: "игрок получит на $2 меньше", and "блэк" (read as "blank" — **inference**) for "empty".
- **Product owner versus engineers:** "Не как бы вам хотелось бы, а как маркет требует", citing Steve Jobs.
- **Naming:** "я не люблю описывать киоск как киоск я бы хотел бы сказать что это флекс пей… быть [консистентны] к нашим стандартам".
- **Standards:** the text library goes to Andrey Bosik under a mandatory standard; standards are small, single-purpose .md files, "сотни и сотни".
- **The date:** "почему нужно ещё 10 дней?" He accepted the team is honest and left it open: "хотел бы видеть другие сроки" ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-goes-with-flex-iq-alone-and-one-kiosk-per-location.md]], Sept-22 update).
- **Tests:** release on the 2nd only if developers help with end-to-end tests; test JVL's own ecosystem first (his Texas two-machine case, with "у нас ещё нету диспенсера"); one one-hour micro-step by tomorrow. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-22-autotests-go-inside-the-existing-capabilities-and-the-first-e2e-test-is-a-one-hour-micro-step.md]].
- **Dependencies:** he asked whether products need regular updates and closed it on the engineers' answer ([[wiki/topics/github-release-management-pipeline.md]], Sept 22).

## Sept 28 2026 (12:50 PM) — lunch with Larry, John and Eddie: the depreciation deal, "with or without you", and the banking upside set aside

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-larry-john-eddie-lunch-transactional-equipment-investment-structure.md|Sept-28 lunch]] (English) with [[wiki/people/larry-sharon.md|Larry]] and his two railcar-investor partners, Val pitched the Georgia transactional equipment as an investment ([[wiki/projects/transactional-equipment-depreciation-investor-deal.md]]) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]]:

- **The model in one sentence.** "We either sell the transactional equipment and collect ninety dollars per month, or we give away the transactional equipment for free and collect one hundred fifty dollars a month." His realistic target: "twenty percent of the market… let's call it two thousand locations. Two thousand locations times four is eight million dollars."
- **He offered the vehicle.** "I can register JVL Distributing in Georgia… where my company and your company will be fifty fifty partners"; the $12,000 balance per unit is "Not financed. I open receivable."
- **He will not wait.** "I'm getting ready to do it with or without you"; if they pass, "at a slower pace, or I'm gonna do it through some other."
- **He set the banking dream aside himself.** "What I'm discussing right now is a complete unknown and complete dream"; "I would also suggest for you to think that banking deal as something that is not going to happen… I feel uber uncomfortable promising something like that." (The dream itself — toll booth, 1–8% on $5 billion, a bank — is on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]], Sept 28; the InComm history on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]], Sept 28.)
- **Licence.** "I'll get you a license… Distributor license is not a problem"; "Thirty days? I don't want to promise"; after Larry's transferability point, "You're right… I don't think you should be investing anything into anything until you hundred percent."
- **Candour about JVL's standing.** "I am between number three and number six" in Georgia, for two reasons he named (on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]], Sept 28); "About sixty-five" employees, "down from one hundred and fifty."

## Sept 28 2026 (3:33 PM) — the same idea to an unnamed tax-investment adviser: "one fourth of a cash", and "I genuinely don't want anything"

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-equipment-depreciation-investor-pitch.md|3:33 PM pitch]] (English) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Equipment_Depreciation_Investor_Pitch-September-28-2026.md]]:

- **"I'm in the exploration stage right now."** The idea: "the new beautiful bill has a hundred percent write-off… They will put in one fourth. The depreciation on the full amount… It's going to be Rev Share… on exit they will get a percentage." He admitted he had only lately seen it: "I never appreciated the opportunity of writing off everything in one year… We're talking about maybe weeks."
- **He would not be pinned on lead time.** "You're asking the wrong question… I am moving with or without you. With or without them… You're not the only person I'm speaking to." Full disclosure at the end: "I'm speaking to not a broker, to another few friends of mine."
- **He declined the adviser's handshake terms.** "I genuinely don't want anything… I absolutely want nothing."
- **The ambition, sized.** "If we get the banking side, a billion-dollar business"; "What I would like to do is own the bank, or we could go into partnership with the bank" (on the prize-card page, Sept 28). And the boundary he keeps: "I'm looking for street gaming… I cannot make money in casinos."
- **A card idea in passing.** He likes a metal card with perks — "airport lounge on me, just like platinum" — and the adviser showed him an NFC tap card as a model. No product decision.

I just never wanted to bring it up yet"; "I knew about problem B before" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Sharon_Lease_Pricing_and_Profit_Repatriation-September-29-2026.md]].
- **His own pricing problem, said plainly.** "Я всем сказал… если ты покупаешь машину, то мы тебе это даем за девяносто долларов сервис-чардж. Но сейчас мне нужно было взять сто пятьдесят долларов" — he told everyone $90 with a machine, now needs $150; a customer's $150 × 60 months does not reach the $15,000 equipment price. "We should charge closer to two hundred dollars. I wouldn't want to… because I would want to penetrate the market" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Sharon_Lease_Pricing_and_Profit_Repatriation-September-29-2026.md]].
- **His first idea, then the lease.** A lifetime contract with all service and advance replacement, exit by paying the rest of the equipment price; then, on Larry's car-lease framing, "Когда мы говорим, что даем это бесплатно, это совсем другая коннотация… So I got stuck on my own bullshit" and "Это ничего за бесплатным мы вам не делаем. Мы делаем за 150 долларов минимум" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Sharon_Lease_Pricing_and_Profit_Repatriation-September-29-2026.md]].
- **On "problem C" (JVL taxed on an unpaid invoice):** "It is a problem… That one is a real problem"; on moving JVL's domicile: "Of course it's impossible. And even if it's possible, we're talking about months, years." On Larry's $4,000-cash, no-note version: "it's much easier. And at the end, we may build a huge business and be very happy" — and then: "put a fire extinguisher over your heat right now and sit down and think for yourself whether you're even interested in that deal" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Pricing_Pivot_to_Cash_and_Revenue_Share-September-29-2026.md]].

## Sept 29 2026 (6:02 AM) — the AI PM design call: "I don't want the AI PM to write to us; I want it to do the work", the bot to MVP 2, and "no bonus — Friday at 7"

He called and ran the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-29-ai-pm-architecture-and-second-brain-plan.md|Sept-29 "Design_AI-PM" call]] (Russian) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_AI_PM_Architecture_and_Second_Brain_Plan-September-29-2026.md]]. The project is on [[wiki/projects/ai-project-manager-and-company-second-brain.md]]; the rulings on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-29-bosik-owns-the-ai-pm-the-meeting-bot-waits-and-the-second-brain-is-built-first.md]].

- **The model is his AI-CFO.** A role he uses "каждый день… [д]овольно успешно", that "опирается только на факты", that talks with the accounting department and has begun to give directions; it runs "как рутин. 2 раза в день" and makes him a report he reacts to. He is now setting up another such role for himself (the name is garbled on tape).
- **What he wants.** "Я не хочу чтобы AIPM нам писал, я хочу чтоб это был рабочий AIP[M], который с нас снимает работу. Его задача [создавать] таски проперные, без ошибок. И в лимитированном количестве. Согласно нашим стандартам." No human "responsible": "Это… должна быть автономная роль… единственный полноценный про[джект] менеджер". Knowledge deliberately narrow — Jira, Confluence, PM textbooks, the company, the standards, the code and the prose, "[н]е давать… никакой другой философии".
- **The second brain, not a knowledge base.** Facts are the frozen conversations; the index is built on them. Transcripts stay in Obsidian with a light local index; summaries go to GitHub — the architecture his Claude recommended and that he offered to repeat for the company. His own brain: 18 months of transcripts, about a month and about $7,000 to process, checked quarterly.
- **Order and pace.** The meeting bot goes to MVP 2; "давайте мы имплементируем сегодня то, что легко заработает" — "[м]ы не хотим наращивать количество людей. Скорость мы хотим повысить". Transcripts into one store first (1–2 days), then the brain (about a month, started first), the persona in parallel (2–3 days), the glossary after.
- **"Никаких бонусов."** To Bosik's "через недельку, через 2" and "Это так бонус": "у меня нету стоков времени, так это не работает… никаких бонусов здесь нету. Давайте в пятницу встретимся… гдето на 07:00."
- **His way with two AIs.** A "teacher" Claude designs and an "implementer" Claude builds; he copies between them (Bosik's term: "мясное прокси"). His advice: "сделайте вид, что вы ничего не знаете… [н]е задавайте тон работы". See [[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]] (Sept 29). At the close he asked that the transcript disregard what he had said about applying AI (recorded on the stub for the owner).

**§15.8:** his remarks about how Bosik presents himself and his general asides are not recorded. **Personal matters not recorded.** **§15 #9:** none.

- **At 10:31 AM** he closed with the AI project manager: "Мне кажется, мы на правильном пути мне понравился этот разговор. ## Sept 30 2026 (8:42 AM) — the Larry call: "No gymnastics whatsoever", "let's have a small test", and "the business will finance itself"

Larry Sharon called with the reworked deal ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-30-larry-sharon-single-entity-jv-and-100-machine-pilot.md]]; mostly English, some Russian). Full detail on [[wiki/projects/transactional-equipment-depreciation-investor-deal.md]] (Sept 30) and the rulings on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-one-jv-entity-a-florida-llc-50-50-with-a-special-allocation-of-depreciation-and-jvl-invoices-the-real-4000.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-a-100-machine-trial-first-then-bell-bank-seacoast-and-possibly-incomm-finance-the-rest.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Sharon_Single_Entity_JV_and_100_Machine_Pilot-September-30-2026.md]]:

- **He accepted at once.** "Unbelievably. Smart… I would have not come up with this"; "Very smart, very clever, very simple. No complications. No gymnastics whatsoever"; and on the real-value invoice, "Even better… We don't have to do. Any Of that."
- **He still thought in the old numbers for a moment.** "I have to. Out invoice for the full amount, so I know there will be income that I need to book a JVL. But I must say I will have to get that fifteen thousand" — Larry: "Why fifteen?… I won't have an invoice for fifteen." Val: "And I got infected by."
- **He asked for the small run.** "I really don't want you to take any risks with me. I hate to bring people that may lose money with me. So let's have a small test, small run, because we will have disappointments"; "Let's call it for the sake of this deal. Let's call it a 100 machine trial"; "We have a handshake agreement. We're ready to go."
- **His own view of financing.** "I actually don't think that we need to finance this business past one thousand machines. Not machines, installations. I think the business will finance itself."
- **InComm is not the only door.** "Income [InComm] has two competitors" — one he could not name ("Blackwater… I might be misquoting"), and "NCR… being purchased right now by Brinks… They have actually a product that we might even bring on board afterwards" — "At least two more parties."

## Sept 30 2026 (10:00 AM) — the GitHub-to-MSBC meeting: "I don't want to be a therapist", "one flat list", and "this meeting is not productive"

He called and ran the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-30-github-to-msbc-bom-flat-list-bridge.md|Sept-30 GitHub_to_MSBC_BOM_Integration meeting]] (English) with Genadi, Nathan and Lev. Rulings on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-engineers-never-change-msbc-and-administration-never-changes-github.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-a-new-part-number-is-assigned-by-claude-from-the-standard-in-github-and-pushed-to-msbc-once.md]]; the contested BOM half on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-23-the-bom-source-of-truth-is-the-latest-design-in-github-and-sync-runs-one-way.md]] (Sept-30 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GitHub_to_MSBC_BOM_Flat_List_Bridge-September-30-2026.md]]:

- **How he set it up.** "I don't want to play a role of a therapist that convinces why one thing is better than the other. I'm just saying that's how we are setting up our business. To function… let's identify a concern and let's find a solution for it."
- **The rule.** "Engineers are never allowed. To change anything in Msbc. The administrator is never allowed to change anything in Github"; later, "a clear border, non-penetrable border, between engineering and administration… under the key and lock."
- **The flat list.** "Genadi says there is 550 nominations that will go into assembling one machine… But a flat list is 550 nominations"; "in Github, it is all flood [flat] information… It gets grouped, compiled… inside of Msbc. But that grouping, the step, could be a step of automation. It doesn't have to be a manual step."
- **On Navision.** "Navision was designed 50 years ago, got obsolete 30 years ago, and was taken offline 20 years ago, and now practically dead… whatever methodology Navision used, we kind of inherited and trained our minds to use."
- **What he does not know.** Asked what exactly Claude will do to populate a part into three products: "Hopefully… I don't know… before Nathan had a chance to experiment with it, we don't know right now, but it's not… spaceship science."
- **He cut it short.** "I think this meeting is not productive. I need to let Lev and Nathan go because I'm just burning their hours. And I will switch to a different meeting with Genadi."

- **4:59 PM — the IO board.** He opened with "Говорим ещё раз, что нужно сделать?" and asked one question — "Что это за чипы, как они называются?" — then let Genadi's three-step plan stand (two FRAM chips off at $12 a board; a half-cost redesign; the street board derived from it): [[wiki/decisions/2026-10-01-two-fram-chips-come-off-the-current-io-board-and-the-street-machine-board-grows-out-of-a-redesigned-io-board.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IO_Board_Memory_Chip_Removal_and_Redesign-October-1-2026.md]].
- **5:02 PM — the BOM, "Я хочу вернуться к разговору".** His framing of the whole thing: exporting a BOM and keeping orders and admin in MSBC "это неправильный подход к теме… убивает процесс того, что… я хочу добиться." His reasons, in order: "я не хочу расти в количестве людей… найти хороших людей всегда было тяжело… мы работаем в разных местах"; he will not build a C-level layer under himself ("какую-то бюрократию, которая совсем для ведения кампании не полезна") — the replacement is AI executives ("у меня сейчас хорошо работает [AI-CFO]… Мы практически закончили архитектуру под AI проджект менеджер"); and supplier history must leave Genadi's head into "свою память, AI… и AI… логику." The ruling: "Туда [в MSBC] нужно загнать только готовый [bill of material]. А конфигуратор… нужно создать вне, он должен быть логикой AI"; "Я не хочу делать гибрид"; prices do not go in GitHub. His sources of truth: the flat BOM in GitHub, lead time and MOQ-type facts, the physical count, the forecast — "Для меня этот [substance] это AI." On MSBC: "это больная форма того, что могли сделать за все это время." He proposed the pilot himself — "возьмём [flat bill of material], у нас не займёт больше, чем [два часа]. Я при вас поиграю с этим" — picked up Genadi's choice of the Slim, asked for the part number in the chat, and said "я попрошу, чтобы [Claude Code] создал мне инфраструктуру… Я практически уверен, что я сам это смогу сделать." On Nathan his line is garbled ("Да мне давно это нэйтане нужен, я сам могу это сделать"). His proof that AI can hold the logic: "сегодня Алексей Хамин и Ваня наш пишут эти тесты. И [Claude] тестируют." Ruling: [[wiki/decisions/2026-10-01-only-the-finished-bom-goes-into-msbc-the-configurator-is-ai-logic-outside-it-no-hybrid-and-the-pilot-is-the-flex-pay-slim.md]]; thread on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-23-the-bom-source-of-truth-is-the-latest-design-in-github-and-sync-runs-one-way.md]] (Oct-1 update); the staffing and autotest points on [[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]] (Oct 1) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flat_BOM_in_GitHub_and_AI_Configurator-October-1-2026.md]].
- **5:39 PM — the street cabinet.** On the FlexPort rear cover: a template and a hole saw, fixings at the sides, "это исключение из правил" for machines whose panel cannot be cut ([[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]], Oct 1). On the cabinet: the dashboard is a service panel; the one real problem is paper loading; then "я предлагаю, чтоб вы практически имплементировали ту же конструкцию [as the Flex]… опустить это пониже. Эти 2 девайса… прямо на панели", "дашборд уменьшить в 2 раза", "всю конструкцию из пластика… Я бы не хотел бы строить металл на это", and to Igor starting now: "Я думаю, что ещё рановато." Then his own new question: "Если стоит [street] машина, то зачем мы туда ставим этот флекспорт? Нам нужно только один [scanner — inference]… перенести на общий экран машины" — "давайте последним ещё подумаю." Ruling: [[wiki/decisions/2026-10-01-the-street-cabinets-printer-and-acceptor-come-down-onto-the-panel-flex-style-in-plastic-and-the-model-does-not-go-to-igor-yet.md]]; the rest on [[wiki/topics/low-cost-street-machine.md]] (Oct 1) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Dashboard_and_Printer_Access-October-1-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** his sharper remarks at Genadi in the BOM segment, an exchange about who is "smart" or "dumb", and a joke he told are held; personal matters not recorded. **§15 #9:** none.

## Open commitments (Oct 1 2026)

- [ ] Val Levitan — take the Oct-1 transcript and the Flex Pay Slim part number and have Claude Code build the GitHub structure: the Slim as a flat list with all suppliers for every part, then work backward — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flat_BOM_in_GitHub_and_AI_Configurator-October-1-2026.md]]
- [ ] Val Levitan and Genadi Peari — a working session of about two hours on the flat BOM, Val working it in front of Genadi — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flat_BOM_in_GitHub_and_AI_Configurator-October-1-2026.md]]
- [ ] Val Levitan — think over whether the street machine needs a FlexPort at all, or one scanner with the interface on the machine's touch screen; blocks the hand-over of the model to Igor — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Dashboard_and_Printer_Access-October-1-2026.md]]

## Sept 30 2026 (in absentia) — about thirty more modems on his order, and a spare-parts email to him

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-30-transactional-mvp1-instructions-complete-and-modem-supply.md|Sept-30 8:03 AM MVP 1 call]] (Russian; he was not present) Pavel, asked whether a new modem batch is on order: "в джорджии [Вал давал]… [в] команду купить помоему 30 новых. И они сейчас как раз покупаются" — about 30 more, the same (end-of-life) model as Georgia's first 10, being bought now. Klimovich will start an email to Genadi and him to bring the Minsk spare-parts set up to date. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-north-america-modem-variant-and-ask-kore-for-the-apn.md]] (Sept-30 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].
